###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:6)


**חָזַר וְנָגַח שׁוֹר שָׁוֶה מָאתַיִם.** הָאַחֲרוֹן נוֹטֵל מָנֶה הַחֵצִי מִכָּל מַה שֶּׁהוּא, דְּמִגּוּפוֹ מִשְׁתַּלֵּם. וְנִמְצָא אוֹתוֹ שֶׁלְּפָנָיו שֶׁהָיָה הַחֵצִי שֶׁלּוֹ, מְשַׁלֵּם מֵחֶלְקוֹ חֲצִי מָנֶה שֶׁנּוֹטֵל הָאַחֲרוֹן:


חזר ונגח שור שוה מאתים – the [owner of the] last one takes a half Maneh from every one – for he is paid out of his own pocket and it is found that the one before him that was his one-half, he pays from his portion one-half a Maneh and takes the last one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:7)


**וּשְׁנַיִם הָרִאשׁוֹנִים.** נִזָּק רִאשׁוֹן וְהַבְּעָלִים שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם לְכָל אֶחָד רְבִיעַ, מְשַׁלְּמִין כָּל אֶחָד רְבִיעִית שֶׁל נִזְקוֹ:


But the two first [owners] – the first owner [whose ox] was damaged and the owners, for each one of them had a quarter, each one of them pays one-quarter of his damages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:1:8)


**דִּינָר זָהָב.** שֶׁהֵם עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה דִּינָרִים שֶׁל כֶּסֶף:


דינר זהב – which is twenty-five silver dinars.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:1)


**מוּעָד לִקְטַנִּים.** לַעֲגָלִים:


הועד לקטנים – to calves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:2)


**אָמְרוּ לִפְנֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה.** שָׁאֲלוּ תַּלְמִידָיו מִמֶּנּוּ:


אמרו לפני ר' יהודה – his students asked of him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:3)


**מוּעָד לְשַׁבָּתוֹת.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא בָּטֵל מִמְּלָאכָה וְדַעְתּוֹ זָחָה עָלָיו. אִי נַמִּי לְפִי שֶׁרוֹאֶה בְּנֵי אָדָם בְּמַלְבּוּשִׁים נָאִים שֶׁל שַׁבָּת, חֲשׁוּבִים בְּעֵינָיו נָכְרִים וְאֵינוֹ מַכִּירָם:


Forewarned for Sabbaths – because he (i.e., the animal) is idle from work and it became overbearing; alternatively, because it sees human beings in their nice Shabbat clothing, they are considered in its eyes as known but he does not recognize them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:4)


**וְאֵינוֹ מוּעָד לְחֹל.** מַה דִּינוֹ:


But he is not forewarned on weekdays, what is the law regarding this?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:2:5)


**מִשֶּׁיַּחֲזֹר בּוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה יְמֵי שַׁבָּתוֹת.** לְאַחַר שֶׁהוּעַד לְשַׁבָּתוֹת הֶעֱבִירוּ לְפָנָיו שְׁוָרִים בְּשָׁלֹשׁ שַׁבָּתוֹת וְלֹא נָגַח, חָזַר לְתַמּוּתוֹ, וְאִם חָזַר וְנָגַח אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא חֲצִי נֶזֶק:


משיחזור בו שלשה שבתות – (see Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 2, Mishnah 4). After it (i.e., the owner of the ox) was forewarned for Sabbaths, they brought before him (i.e., the animal) oxen on three Sabbaths and it did not gore, it returned to its being innocuous, and if it returned to being innocuous and it gored, it (i.e., the owner) only pays one-half damages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:3:1)


**שׁוֹר שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנָּגַח שׁוֹר שֶׁל נָכְרִי פָּטוּר.** דִּכְתִיב (חבקוק ג) עָמַד וַיְמֹדֶד אֶרֶץ רָאָה וַיַּתֵּר גּוֹיִם, רָאָה שֶׁבַע מִצְוֹת שֶׁנִּצְטַוּוּ בְּנֵי נֹחַ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא קִיְּמוּ אוֹתָן, עָמַד וְהִתִּיר מָמוֹנָן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְאוֹמֵר (דברים לג) הוֹפִיעַ מֵהַר פָּארָן, גִּלָּה מָמוֹנָן שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וְהִתִּירָן. מֵהַר פָּארָן, מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁסִּבֵּב וְהֶחְזִיר אֶת הַתּוֹרָה עַל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וְלֹא קִבְּלוּהָ:


שור של ישראל שנגח שור של עובד כוכבים פטור – as it is written (Habbakuk 3:6): “When He stands, He makes the earth shake; When He glances, He makes nations tremble.” He saw the seven commandments that were commanded to the Sons of Noah. Since they did not fulfill them, He (i.e., God) stood and He released their money to Israel, and it states (Deuteronomy 33:2): “He appeared from Mount Paran [and approached from Ribeboth-kodesh},” He (i.e., God) revealed the money of the idolaters and permitted it [to the Jewish people]. “From Mount Paran”: from the time that he went around from one to the other offering the idolaters the Torah and they did not accept it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:1)


**וְשֶׁל חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן שֶׁנָּגַח שׁוֹר שֶׁל פִּקֵּחַ פָּטוּר.** שֶׁאֵין מַעֲמִידִין אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס לְתָם לִגְבּוֹת מִגּוּפוֹ, דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי הוּא, וְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא [דַּף יד] שָׁוֶה כֶּסֶף מְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵין בֵּית דִּין נִזְקָקִים אֶלָּא לִנְכָסִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן אַחֲרָיוּת, וְאוֹקִימְנָא בְּיַתְמֵי:


ושל חרש שוטה וקטן שנגח שור של פקח פטור – for we don’t appoint an administrator/guardian for an innocuous ox to collect [damages] from his estate for they are movables, and we stated in the first chapter (Tractate Bava Kamma 14b) an equivalent amount of money teaches that the Jewish court does not need anything other than property from which debts may eventually be collected and we establish this for orphans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:2)


**מַעֲמִידִין לָהֶן אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס וְכוּ'.** אִם הֻחְזְקוּ נַגְּחָנִים, מַעֲמִידִין לָהֶם אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס, וְלֹא לְשַׁלֵּם חֲצִי נֶזֶק אֶלָּא לְשַׁוְּיֵהּ מוּעָד, דְּכִי הָדַר נָגַח מְשַׁלֵּם מִן הָעֲלִיָּה וְיִגְבּוּ הַנֶּזֶק מִקַּרְקַע שֶׁל יְתוֹמִים:


מעמידין להן אפוטרופוסים וכו' – if they (i.e., oxen) are known to be gorers, we appoint for them a guardian/administrator and not to pay one-half damage other than for forewarned oxen, for if it gores again, they pay from the most valuable property and they collect the damages from the land of orphans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:3)


**חָזַר לְתַמּוּתוֹ.** דְּקָסָבַר מוּעָד שֶׁיָּצָא מֵרְשׁוּת בְּעָלָיו וְנִכְנַס לִרְשׁוּת בְּעָלִים אֲחֵרִים חוֹזֵר לְתַמּוּתוֹ. דִּרְשׁוּת מְשֻׁנָּה מְשַׁנָּה אֶת דִּין הַתְרָאָתוֹ:


חוזר לתמותו – for he (i.e., Rabbi Meir) holds that if it (i.e., the animal) left the domain of his masters an entered into the domain of other masters, he returns to his [former status] of innocuousness, for a different domain changes the law of its being “forewarned.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:4:4)


**שׁוֹר הָאִצְטָדִין.** שֶׁמְּיֻחָד לִנְגִיחוֹת, וּמְלַמְּדִין אוֹתוֹ לְכָךְ:


שור האיצטדין – that is designated for goring and they (i.e., the oxen) are instructed in this.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:5:1)


**מוּעָד מְשַׁלֵּם כֹּפֶר.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְקַמָּא דְּנָגַח קָטְלִינַן לֵיהּ, אַשְׁכְּחִינַן מוּעָד, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָרַג שְׁלֹשָׁה נָכְרִים. אִי נַמִּי, שֶׁהָרַג שְׁלֹשָׁה יִשְׂרָאֵל טְרֵפָה, דְּאַטְּרֵפָה לֹא קָטְלִינַן לֵיהּ, דְּגַבְרָא קְטִילָא קָטֵיל. אִי נַמִּי, דְּקָטֵיל וַעֲרַק לְאַגְמָא לְאַחַר שֶׁהֵעִידוּ בּוֹ:


מועד משלם כופר – and even though that in the first that he (i.e., the ox) gored, we kill it, we find him [now] to be forewarned, as in the example of one that killed three idolaters; alternatively, that it killed three Israelites, it is considered “torn” (as by another animal), for something that is “torn”, we don’t kill it as a man put to death is considered as dead; alternatively, someone who is [sentenced to be] killed and flees to the lake after they testified against him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:5:2)


**וְכֵן בְּבֵן וְכֵן בְּבַת.** תִּינוֹק וְתִינוֹקֶת, חַיָּב עֲלֵיהֶן סְקִילָה וְכֹפֶר כִּגְדוֹלִים:


וכן בבן וכן בבת – small male child and small female child, he is liable for stoning and ransom like for adults.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:6:1)


**שׁוֹר שֶׁהָיָה מִתְחַכֵּךְ בְּכֹתֶל וְכוּ'.** פָּטוּר מִן הַמִּיתָה. וְאִם הָיָה מוּעָד, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה מוּעָד לְהִתְחַכֵּךְ בִּכְתָלִים וּלְהַפִּילָם עַל בְּנֵי אָדָם, וְנִתְחַכֵּךְ בְּכֹתֶל לַהֲנָאָתוֹ וְהִפִּילוֹ עַל הָאָדָם וָמֵת, הַשּׁוֹר פָּטוּר מִן הַמִּיתָה, וְהַבְּעָלִים מְשַׁלְּמִים אֶת הַכֹּפֶר. הַשּׁוֹר פָּטוּר מִן הַמִּיתָה, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) הַשּׁוֹר יִסָּקֵל וְגַם בְּעָלָיו יוּמַת, כְּמִיתַת הַבְּעָלִים כָּךְ מִיתַת הַשּׁוֹר, מַה בְּעָלִים אֵין חַיָּבִין אִם הָרְגוּ אֶת הַנֶּפֶשׁ עַד שֶׁיַּהַרְגוּ בְּכַוָּנָה, כָּךְ הַשּׁוֹר אֵינוֹ חַיָּב עַד שֶׁיַּהֲרֹג בְּכַוָּנָה. וְהַבְּעָלִים מְשַׁלְּמִים אֶת הַכֹּפֶר, דִּכְתִיב אִם כֹּפֶר, שֶׁהָיָה יָכוֹל לִכְתֹּב כֹּפֶר יוּשַׁת עָלָיו, מַאי אִם כֹּפֶר, לְרַבּוֹת הַהוֹרֵג שֶׁלֹּא בְּכַוָּנָה לְחִיּוּב כֹּפֶר:


שור שהיה מתחכך בכותל וכו' – is exempt from the death penalty, but if if was forewarned, as in the case that it was forewarned to rub itself against walls and to knock them over on humans, and it rubbed itself against the wall for its pleasure and it fell on a human being and he died, the ox is exempt from the death penalty but the owners pay the ransom. The ox is exempt from the death penalty, as it is written (Exodus 21:29): “[If, however, that ox has been in the habit of goring, and is owner, though warned, has failed to guard it, and it kills a man or a woman -] the ox shall be stoned, and its owner, too, shall be put to death.” As it is with the death of the owners, so too with the death of the ox. Just as the owners are not liable if they killed a person, until they killed him with intent, so too, the ox is not liable until it kills with intent. And the owners pay ransom, as it is written (Exodus 21:30): “If ransom is laid upon him, [he must pay whatever is laid upon him to redeem his life],” for the Torah could have stated



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:1)


**שׁוֹר הַיְתוֹמִים.** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס:


ושור היתומים – that lack an administrator/guardian.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:2)


**וְשׁוֹר הָאַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס.** הַשּׁוֹר שֶׁל יְתוֹמִים הוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁעַל הָאַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס לְשָׁמְרוֹ:


ושור האפוטרופוס – it is the ox of orphans, but it is upon the guardian/administrator to guard it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:3)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ חַיָּבִין.** דְּשִׁבְעָה שׁוֹר כְּתוּבִים בַּפָּרָשָׁה בְּנוֹגֵחַ אָדָם. חַד לְגוּפֵיהּ, וְשִׁשָּׁה לְשִׁשָּׁה שְׁוָרִים הַלָּלוּ:


הרי אלו חייבין – for seven “oxen” are written in the portion of goring a person (i.e., Exodus 21;28-30), one for itself and [the other] six for these six oxen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:7:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר שׁוֹר הֶקְדֵּשׁ שׁוֹר הַגֵּר שֶׁמֵּת וְאֵין לוֹ יוֹרְשִׁים פְּטוּרִים מִן הַמִּיתָה.** אֲפִלּוּ נָגַח וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִקְדִּישׁ, נָגַח וְאַחַר כָּךְ מֵת הַגֵּר, הָיָה פּוֹטֵר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שור הקדש שור הגר שמת ואין לו יורשים. פטורים מן שמיתה – even if it gored and afterwards was dedicated to the Temple; it gored and afterwards the proselyte died, Rabbi Yehuda would declare it exempt [from the death penalty – as they have no owners). But thee Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:8:1)


**וְאִם שְׁחָטוֹ אָסוּר.** בַּאֲכִילָה. דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) סָקוֹל יִסָּקֵל הַשּׁוֹר וְלֹא יֵאָכֵל אֶת בְּשָׂרוֹ, מִמַּשְׁמַע שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר סָקוֹל יִסָּקֵל הַשּׁוֹר אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהִיא נְבֵלָה וּנְבֵלָה אֲסוּרָה בַּאֲכִילָה, וּמַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְלֹא יֵאָכֵל אֶת בְּשָׂרוֹ, אֶלָּא לוֹמַר לְךָ שֶׁאִם קָדַם וּשְׁחָטוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְמַר דִּינוֹ אָסוּר:


ואם שחטו אסור – [if he slaughtered it, he is prohibited from] eating it, as it is written (Exodus 21:28): “The ox shall be stoned and its flesh shall not be eaten, [but the owner of the ox is not to be punished].”From the plain meaning of the verse, it implies, as it is stated, “the ox shall be stoned” – don’t I know that it is considered as carrion and carrion is forbidden to be eaten? What then is to be learned here for the verse to say “and its flesh shall not be eaten”- but rather to say to you, that if he advanced to kill it after judgement had been passed upon it, it is forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:8:2)


**הִקְדִּישׁוֹ בְעָלָיו מֻקְדָּשׁ.** וְנָפְקָא מִנַּהּ דְּאִי מִתְהַנֵי מִנֵּיהּ מָעַל:


הקדישו בעליו מוקדש – and we learn from this that if he derived benefit from it, he has committed religious sacrilege.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:1)


**וְנָעַל בְּפָנָיו כָּרָאוּי.** בְּדֶלֶת שֶׁיְּכוֹלָה לַעֲמֹד בְּרוּחַ מְצוּיָה. וְהַיְנוּ שְׁמִירָה פְּחוּתָה:


ונעל בפניו כראוי – [locked it up before him] with a door that can withstand an ordinary wind, and that is identical with a lesser guarding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:2)


**וּמוּעָד פָּטוּר.** מִצַּד הַעֲדָאָה שֶׁבּוֹ. אֲבָל צַד תַּמּוּת בִּמְקוֹמָהּ עוֹמֶדֶת. וּמְשַׁלֵּם חֲצִי נֶזֶק כְּתָם. דִּכְתִיב וְלֹא יִשְׁמְרֶנּוּ גַּבֵּי מוּעָד, הָא שְׁמָרוֹ כָּל דְּהוּ פָּטוּר מִצַּד הַעֲדָאָה:


ומועד פטור – from the side of the law concerning damages payable after warning was given to him, but the side of that it should die stands in his place, and he (i.e., the owner) pays half-damages like an innocuous ox, as it is written (Exodus 21:29): “[and its owner, though warned,] has failed to guard it,” regarding a forewarned ox. But if he had guarded it/watched it a little bit, he is exempt from the side of the law concerning damages payable after warning to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/4:9:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר, אֵין לוֹ שְׁמִירָה.** לַמּוּעָד אֶלָּא סַכִּין. עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁחֲטֶנּוּ. וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחֲלֹקוֹת בַּדָּבָר. לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר בִּשְׁמִירָה פְּחוּתָה חַיָּב וּבִמְעֻלָּה פָּטוּר. וּלְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בִּשְׁמִירָה פְּחוּתָה נַמִּי פָּטוּר מִצַּד הַעֲדָאָה שֶׁבּוֹ, אֲבָל חַיָּב עַל צַד תַּמּוּת שֶׁבּוֹ, עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁמְרֶנּוּ שְׁמִירָה מְעֻלָּה. וּלְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בִּמְעֻלָּה נַמִּי חַיָּב. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וּמִיהוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה מִצְוָה לִשְׁחֹט שׁוֹר הַמּוּעָד כְּדֵי לְסַלֵּק הַהֶזֵּק:


רבי אליעזר אומר – there is no guarding for a forewarned ox other than a knife,



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:1)


**שׁוֹר שֶׁנָּגַח אֶת הַפָּרָה.** מְעֻבֶּרֶת:


שור שנגח את הפרה – who was pregnant.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:2)


**וְנִמְצָא עֻבָּרָהּ בְּצִדָּהּ.** מֵת:


ונמצא עוברת בצדה – that died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:3)


**וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם עַד שֶׁלֹּא נְגָחָהּ יָלְדָה.** וְלֹא מֵת מֵחֲמַת הַנְּגִיחָה:


ואין ידוע אם עד שלא נגח ילדה – and did not die because of the goring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:4)


**אִם מִשֶּׁנְּגָחָהּ.** וּמֵחֲמַת הַנְּגִיחָה הִפִּילָתוֹ:


אם משנגחה – and because of the goring aborted the fetus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:5)


**וּרְבִיעַ נֶזֶק לַוָּלָד.** דְּתָם חַיָּב חֲצִי נֶזֶק, וְהַאי וָלָד מֻטָּל בְּסָפֵק הוּא, וְחוֹלְקִין. וּמַתְנִיתִין סוּמְכוֹס הִיא דְּאָמַר מָמוֹן הַמֻּטָּל בְּסָפֵק חוֹלְקִין. אֲבָל חֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים זֶה כְּלָל גָּדוֹל בַּדִּין, הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וַאֲפִלּוּ נִזָּק אוֹמֵר בָּרִי וּמַזִּיק אוֹמֵר שֶׁמָּא, הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה:


ורביע נזק – for an innocuous ox is liable for half damages and this offspring lies in doubt and we divide it. And our Mishnah is according to Sumachos who stated that money that is lies in doubt, we divide the money. But the Sages state that this is a great principle in law (see, for example, Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 3, Mishnah 11 above): “He who seeks reparation from his fellow must produce evidence (i.e., upon him is the burden of proof).” But the Halakha is according to the Sages. And even if the [owner of the] animal who suffered damages asserts a certainty (i.e., literally, “sure”) and the [owner of the] animal who caused the damages says “perhaps” (i.e., a possibility), “he who seeks reparation form his fellow must produce evidence.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:1:6)


**וְכֵן פָּרָה שֶׁנָּגְחָה אֶת הַשּׁוֹר וְכוּ'‏ מְשַׁלֵּם חֲצִי נֶזֶק מִן הַפָּרָה.** אִם נִמְצֵאת הַפָּרָה, כְּדִין תָּם שֶׁמְּשַׁלֵּם חֲצִי נֶזֶק מִגּוּפוֹ. וְאִם לֹא נִמְצֵאת הַפָּרָה, מְשַׁלֵּם רְבִיעַ נֶזֶק מִן הַוָּלָד. שֶׁאִם הָיָה יָדוּעַ שֶׁקֹּדֶם שֶׁיָּלְדָה נָגְחָה הָיָה מְשַׁלֵּם כָּל חֲצִי נֶזֶק מִן הַוָּלָד, שֶׁמְּעֻבֶּרֶת שֶׁנָּגְחָה הִיא וְעֻבָּרָהּ נָגְחוּ. וְאִם לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּלְדָה נָגְחָה לֹא הָיָה מְשַׁלֵּם מִן הַוָּלָד כְּלוּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַוָּלָד לֹא נָגַח וְאֵין הַתָּם מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא מִגּוּפוֹ. עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁהוּא בְּסָפֵק, חוֹלְקִים, וּמִן הַחֲצִי נֶזֶק שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ לְשַׁלֵּם מְשַׁלֵּם רְבִיעַ נֶזֶק מִן הַוָּלָד:


וכן פרה שנגחה את השור וכו'- if the cow is found, it is according to the innocuous [animal whose owner] pays one-half damages from his estate, but if the cow is not found, he pays one-firth damages from the offspring, for if he had known that prior to [the cow] giving birth it had gored, he would pay all of the half-damages from the offspring, for she was pregnant and had gored and her fetus was gored, but if it was after she gave birth that she gored, he would not pay from the offspring anything, for the offspring did not gore. But the [owner of the] innocuous animal pays other than from his estate. But now that there is a doubt, they divide it and from the one-half damages that he has to pay, he pays one quarter damages from the offspring.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:2:1)


**וְאִם הֻזְּקָה בָהֶם בַּעַל הַפֵּרוֹת חַיָּב.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי שֶׁהֻחְלְקָה וְנִכְשְׁלָה בָּהֶן. אֲבָל אִם אָכְלָה מֵהֶן עַד שֶׁמֵּתָה, בַּעַל הַפֵּרוֹת פָּטוּר, דַּהֲוָה לַהּ שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאכַל:


ואם הוזקה בהם בעל הפירות חייב – and these words refer to when the animal slips and stumbles/falls through them. But if she (i.e., the animal) ate from them until she died , the owner of the produce is exempt [from indemnity] because she should not have eaten them.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:1)


**הָיָה אָבִיו אוֹ בְנוֹ.** שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת בְּתוֹכוֹ. וְהוּא הַדִּין לִשְׁאָר אֲנָשִׁים, אֶלָּא אוֹרְחָא דְּמִלְּתָא נָקַט:


היה אביו או בנו – of the owner of the house were in it (i.e., the cistern) and the same law applies for the rest of the people, but [the Mishnah] took a usual incident.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:2)


**מְשַׁלֵּם אֶת הַכֹּפֶר.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה מוּעָד לְהַפִּיל עַצְמוֹ עַל בְּנֵי אָדָם בְּבוֹרוֹת, וְהָאִידְנָא חָזֵי יַרְקָא בַּבּוֹר וְהִפִּיל עַצְמוֹ לַבּוֹר לֶאֱכֹל יַרְקָא וְהָרַג אֶת הָאָדָם, שֶׁהַשּׁוֹר פָּטוּר מִמִּיתָה הוֹאִיל וְהָרַג שֶׁלֹּא בְּכַוָּנָה, וְהַבְּעָלִים מְשַׁלְּמִין אֶת הַכֹּפֶר, דְּאִתְרַבֵּי כֹּפֶר אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁהָרַג שֶׁלֹּא בְּכַוָּנָה כִּדְאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל:


משלם את הכופר – as for example, the [owner of the] animal had been forewarned to cause himself to fall upon on people in cisterns and currently saw herbs in the pit and caused itself to fall into the cistern to eat the herbs and killed a person, that the [owner of the] ox is exempt from the death penalty, for it killed without intention. But the owners [of the ox] pay the ransom, for it includes the ransom even if the animal killed without intention, as we have stated above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:3:3)


**רַבִּי אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי. הִלְכָּךְ אִם הִכְנִיס שׁוֹרוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת סְתָם וְלֹא קִבֵּל עָלָיו לִשְׁמֹר, בַּעַל הַבַּיִת פָּטוּר, דְּהָא לֹא קִבֵּל עֲלֵיהּ נְטִירוּתָא. וְהַמַּכְנִיס נַמִּי פָּטוּר, לְפִי שֶׁהִכְנִיס בִּרְשׁוּת:


רבי אומר וכו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi [Yehuda HaNasi]. Therefore, if [the owner of] the animal led his ox [into the courtyard] with the permission of the owner of house, in an undefined manner, and he didn’t accept upon himself to guard [the animal], the owner of the house is exempt, for he did not accept upon himself watching/proper care. And the one who brings it (i.e., the animal) in is also exempt [from liability] because he brought it (i.e., the animal) in with permission.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:1)


**שׁוֹר שֶׁהָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לַחֲבֵרוֹ וְכוּ'.** אַיְדֵי דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי סֵיפָא אָדָם שֶׁהָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לַחֲבֵרוֹ, תָּנָא רֵישָׁא נַמִּי שׁוֹר שֶׁהָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לַחֲבֵרוֹ. דַּאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לָאִשָּׁה, פָּטוּר הַשּׁוֹר מִדְּמֵי וְלָדוֹת, שֶׁאֵין חַיָּב בִּדְמֵי וְלָדוֹת אֶלָּא אָדָם בִּלְבַד:


שור שהיה מתכוון לחבירו וכו' – since our Mishnah had to teach the “concluding” (actually, the next phrase) part [of the Mishnah] that a person who intended [to strike] his fellow, the first part [of the Mishnah] teaches also regarding an ox that intends [to gore] its fellow [ox], for even if it had intended [to gore] a woman , the [owner of the] ox is exempt from the payment [for the value of] the offspring, for he is not liable for the payment [for the value of] offspring other than of a human being alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:2)


**אִם כֵּן מִשֶּׁהָאִשָּׁה יוֹלֶדֶת מַשְׁבַּחַת.** אִם כֵּן דְּכָךְ שָׁמִין כִּדְקָאָמַרְתְּ, אִשְׁתָּכַח דְּלֹא יָהֵיב לֵיהּ מִידֵי, שֶׁהֲרֵי מִשֶּׁהָאִשָּׁה יוֹלֶדֶת מַשְׁבַּחַת, שֶׁדָּמֶיהָ פְּחוּתִין לִמָּכֵר קֹדֶם שֶׁתֵּלֵד, שֶׁמְּסֻכֶּנֶת הִיא לָמוּת בְּצַעַר הַלֵּידָה:


א"כ משהאשה יולדת משבחת – if so, that this is what we assess just as you said, it is found that he doesn’t give you anything for from when the woman gives birth, her worth is enhanced for her monetary value is less to be sold prior to her giving birth when she is in danger of dying in the pain of childbirth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:4:3)


**הָיְתָה שִׁפְחָה וְנִשְׁתַּחְרְרָה.** כְּלוֹמַר הָיְתָה מְשֻׁחְרֶרֶת נְשׂוּאָה לְגֵר אוֹ לְעֶבֶד מְשֻׁחְרָר, אוֹ גִּיּוֹרֶת נְשׂוּאָה לְאֶחָד מֵהֶן, וּמֵת הַבַּעַל, פָּטוּר. דְּהַמַּחֲזִיק בְּנִכְסֵי הַגֵּר שֶׁמֵּת וְאֵין לוֹ יֹרְשִׁים, זָכָה, וְזֶה קוֹדֵם לִזְכּוֹת בְּמַה שֶּׁבְּיָדוֹ. וְהוּא הַדִּין נַמִּי לְיִשְׂרְאֵלִית הַנְּשׂוּאָה לְגֵר וּמֵת הַגֵּר, דְּפָטוּר, דְּהָא דְּמֵי וְלָדוֹת לַבַּעַל. אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דִּסְתָם מְשֻׁחְרֶרֶת וְגִיּוֹרֶת נְשׂוּאוֹת לְגֵר וְלִמְשֻׁחְרָר, לְהָכִי נָקַט שִׁפְחָה וְגִיּוֹרֶת:


היתה שפחה ונשתחררה – meaning to say, when she was freed, she was married to a convert or to a manumitted slave or a female convert is married to one of them , and the husband (who was a freed slave or a convert) passes away, [the owner of the one who caused the damages] is exempt, for the individual who takes possession of the property of the convert who died and he has no heirs has merited, and this comes prior to acquiring what is in his hands. And the same law applies also to an Israelite woman who married a convert, and the convert died, he is exempt, for the value of the offspring goes to the husband, but because an undefined freed woman slave or a female convert are married to a male convert or to a freed slave, for this reason, [the Mishnah] took the language of a maidservant and a female convert.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:1)


**בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וּפְתָחוֹ לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד אַחֶרֶת.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין כָּאן צַד רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, חַיָּב. וּבִלְבַד אִם הִפְקִיר רְשׁוּתוֹ, אוֹתָהּ שֶׁפִּי הַבּוֹר לְתוֹכָהּ:


ברשות היחיד ופתו לרשות היחיד אחרת – even though there is not side of a public domain here, he is liable, and only as long as he had made his domain ownerless where the mouth of the cistern is within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:2)


**אֶחָד הַחוֹפֵר בּוֹר.** הוּא עָשׂוּי עָגֹל:


אחד החופר בור – it is made round.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:3)


**שִׁיחַ.** אָרֹךְ וְקָצָר:


שיח – long and narrow [trench].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:4)


**מְעָרָה.** מְרֻבַּעַת וּמְכֻסָּה בְּקֵרוּי, אֶלָּא שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ פֶּה:


מערה – square and covered temporarily but it has an opening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:5)


**חֲרִיצִין.** רְחָבִים וּמְרֻבָּעִים כִּמְעָרָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵינָן מְקֹרִין:


חריצין – wide and squared like a cavern, but they are not its source.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:6)


**נְעִיצִין.** קְצָרִין מִלְּמַטָּה וּרְחָבִים מִלְּמַעְלָה:


נעיצין – short from the bottom and wide from the top.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:5:7)


**מַה בּוֹר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ כְדֵי לְהָמִית עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** דִּסְתָם בּוֹר הוּא גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים:


מה בור שיש בו כדי להמית י' טפחים – for an undefined cistern is ten handbreadths high.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:1)


**הַשֵּׁנִי חַיָּב.** וְהוּא שֶׁמָּסַר לוֹ רִאשׁוֹן לַשֵּׁנִי כְּשֶׁהָלַךְ כִּסּוּי הַבּוֹר לְכַסּוֹתוֹ וְלֹא כִּסָּהוּ הַשֵּׁנִי:


השני חייב – when he (i.e., the first of the partners passed to him while he was walking the cover to cover it and the second one didn’t cover it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:2)


**כִּסָּהוּ כָרָאוּי וְנָפַל לְתוֹכוֹ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִתְלִיעַ הַכִּסּוּי:


כסתו כראוי ונפל לתוכה – such as the case of where the cover became worm-eaten/decayed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:3)


**נָפַל לְפָנָיו מִקּוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה חַיָּב.** שֶׁהָיָה חוֹפֵר בַּבּוֹר וְשָׁמַע הַשּׁוֹר קוֹל פַּטִּישׁ וְנִבְעַת וְנָפַל בַּבּוֹר וָמֵת, חַיָּב. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּכֵיוָן דְּמִשּׁוּם גְּרָמָא דְּקוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה נָפַל הֲוָה לָן לְמֵימַר דְּאִסְתַּלַּק חִיּוּבָא דְּבוֹר מִנֵּיהּ וְרַמְיָא אַפְּשִׁיעוּתָא דְּקוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה וְהַהוּא גְּרָמָא בְּעָלְמָא וּפָטוּר, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי חַיָּב, הוֹאִיל וּבְתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר נִמְצָא הַנֶּזֶק:


נפל לפניו מקול הכרייה חייב – he was digging in the pit and the ox heard the sound of a hammer and was frightened and fell into the pit and died, he [the owner of the pit] is liable and even though that since it was because of indirect effect of the sound of the digging that it fell, we could say that the liability of the pit goes away from it and throws [instead] on the negligence regarding the sound of the digging but this is a mere indirect cause and he is exempt. Nevertheless, he is liable since the damage was found inside the pit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:4)


**לְאַחֲרָיו מִקּוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה פָּטוּר.** אִם נִכְשַׁל הַשּׁוֹר מִקּוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה עַל שְׂפַת הַבּוֹר וְנָפַל לְאַחֲרָיו חוּץ לַבּוֹר וָמֵת, פָּטוּר. שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נִמְצָא הַנֶּזֶק בַּבּוֹר, וְקוֹל הַכְּרִיָּה גְּרָמָא בְּעָלְמָא הוּא וּפָטוּר:


לאחריו מקול הכרייה פטור – if the ox stumbled/fell from the sound of the digging on the edge of the pit and fell backwards outside of the pit and died, he (i.e., the owner of the pit) is exempt [from liability], for the damage was not found in the pit and the sound of the digging was a mere indirect cause and he is exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:5)


**וְנִשְׁתַּבְּרוּ, וְנִתְקָרְעוּ.** בִּכְלֵי שׁוֹר שַׁיָּךְ שְׁבִירָה, הָעֹל וְהַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה. בִּכְלֵי חֲמוֹר שַׁיָּךְ קְרִיעָה, חֲבִילַת בְּגָדִים וּמַרְדַּעַת שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו:


ונשתברו. ונתקרעו – with the ox’s utensil belongs the breaking of the yoke and the plough. Regarding the donkey’s utensil belongs the tearing of the package of clothing and the pack saddle/cushion that is on its back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:6)


**וּפָטוּר עַל הַכֵּלִים.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וְנָפַל שָׁמָּה שׁוֹר אוֹ חֲמוֹר, שׁוֹר וְלֹא אָדָם, חֲמוֹר וְלֹא כֵּלִים:


ופטור על הכלים – as it is written (Exodus 21:33): “and an ox or a donkey falls into it.” An ox, but not a human being; a donkey but not utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:6:7)


**שׁוֹר חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן חַיָּב.** שׁוֹר שֶׁהוּא חֵרֵשׁ וְשֶׁהוּא שׁוֹטֶה וְשֶׁהוּא קָטָן, חַיָּב עָלָיו אִם נָפַל לַבּוֹר. אֲבָל שׁוֹר שֶׁהוּא פִּקֵּחַ אֵינוֹ חַיָּב עָלָיו, דְּאִיבָּעֵי לֵיהּ לְעַיּוֹנֵי וּמֵיזָל:


An ox (I.e., its owner) who is deaf-mute, or is an imbecile or is a minor, is liable for it if it fell into the pit. But an ox (i.e., its owner) which can hear is not liable for it, for he needs to investigate/deliberate and continue.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:1)


**אֶחָד שׁוֹר וְאֶחָד כָּל בְּהֵמָה לִנְפִילַת הַבּוֹר.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) כֶּסֶף יָשִׁיב לִבְעָלָיו, לְכָל מִידֵי דְּאִית לֵיהּ בְּעָלִים:


אחד שור ואחד כל בהמה לנפילת הבור – as it is written (Exodus 21:34): “[The one responsible for the pit must make restitution.] He shall pay the price to the owner, [but he shall keep the dead animal],” anything that has owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:2)


**וּלְהַפְרָשַׁת הַר סִינַי.** דִּכְתִיב (שם יט) אִם בְּהֵמָה אִם אִישׁ לֹא יִחְיֶה, וְחַיָּה בִּכְלַל בְּהֵמָה. אִם, לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הָעוֹפוֹת:


ולהפרשת הר סיני – as it is written (Exodus 19:13): “[No hand shall touch him, but he shall be either stoned or shot;] beast or man he shall not live;” and wildlife is included in animals, if also to include birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:3)


**וּלְתַשְׁלוּמֵי כֶפֶל.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כב) עַל כָּל דְּבַר פֶּשַׁע, כָּל מִידֵי דְּבַר פְּשִׁיעָה הוּא:


לתשלומי כפל – as it is written (Exodus 22:8): “In all charges of misappropriation” – anything where there is negligence.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:4)


**וְלַהֲשָׁבַת אֲבֵדָה.** לְכָל אֲבֵדַת אָחִיךָ (דברים כב):


להשבת אבידה – “and so too shall you do with anything that your fellow loses” (Deuteronomy 22:3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:5)


**לִפְרִיקָה.** עֲזֹב תַּעֲזֹב עִמּוֹ (שמות כג). אַף עַל גַּב דִּכְתִיב (שם) חֲמוֹר שׂוֹנַאֲךָ, כָּל בְּהֵמָה בִּכְלָל, דְּיָלְפִינַן חֲמוֹר חֲמוֹר מִשַּׁבָּת שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ (דברים ה) שׁוֹרְךָ וַחֲמוֹרְךָ וְכָל בְּהֶמְתֶּךָ:


לפריקה – (Exodus 23:5): “and would refrain from raising it.” Even though it Is written (Exodus 23:5): “When you see the ass of your enemy [lying under its burden],” all animals are included for we derive "חמור" "חמור" – from Shabbat, as it states (Deuteronomy 5:14): “your ox, your ass, or any of your cattle.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:6)


**לַחֲסִימָה.** לֹא תַּחְסֹם שׁוֹר בְּדִישׁוֹ (שם כה), יָלְפִינַן שׁוֹר שׁוֹר מִשַּׁבָּת:


לחסימה – “You shall not muzzle an ox in its threshing” (Deuteronomy 25:4). We derive "שור" "שור" through a Gezerah Shavah (i.e., analogy) from Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:7)


**לְכִלְאַיִם.** דְּהַרְבָּעָה, אַף עַל גַּב דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יט) בְּהֶמְתְּךָ לֹא תַּרְבִּיעַ כִּלְאַיִם, הָוֵי נַמִּי חַיָּה וָעוֹף בִּכְלָל, דְּיָלְפִינַן בְּהֵמָה בְּהֵמָה מִשַּׁבָּת. וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן כִּלְאַיִם דְּהַנְהָגָה דְּלֹא תַּחֲרֹשׁ בְּשׁוֹר וּבַחֲמוֹר יַחְדָּו (דברים כב) יָלְפִינַן שׁוֹר שׁוֹר מִשַּׁבָּת, דְּהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל שְׁנֵי מִינִים שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף. וּמִיהוּ לְעִנְיַן פְּסַק הַהֲלָכָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁחוֹרֵשׁ וּמַנְהִיג בִּשְׁנֵי מִינִים שֶׁאֶחָד טָמֵא וְאֶחָד טָהוֹר, דּוּמְיָא דְּשׁוֹר וַחֲמוֹר, אֶלָּא שֶׁאָסְרוּ חֲכָמִים כָּל שְׁנֵי מִינִים, בֵּין שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם טְמֵאִים בֵּין שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם טְהוֹרִים:


לכלאים – the (forbidden) coupling of heterogenous animals, even though it is written (Leviticus 19:19): “You shall not let your cattle mate with a different kind.” This is also wildlife and birds included, for we derive "בהמה" "בהמה from Shabbat. And the same law applies to all two kinds of cattle, wildlife and birds. But, however, regarding the legal decision of the Halakha from the Torah, he is not liable other than someone who ploughs or leads with the two species [of same kind of animal] where one is ritually impure and the other is ritually pure, similar to an ox and an ass, but the Sages stated that [it means] any two species whether they are both ritually impure or whether they are ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:8)


**וּלְשַׁבָּת.** דִּכְתִיב שׁוֹרְךָ וַחֲמוֹרְךָ וְכָל בְּהֶמְתֶּךָ, וְחַיָּה בִּכְלַל בְּהֵמָה. וְכָל, רִבּוּיָא הוּא, לְרַבּוֹת עוֹפוֹת:


לשבת – as it is written (Deuteronomy 5:14): “your ox, or your ass, or any of your cattle.” But wildlife is within the category of cattle and every extension of scope/amplification is to include birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 5:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/5:7:9)


**שֶׁדִּבֵּר הַכָּתוּב בַּהֹוֶה.** בְּדָבָר הָרָגִיל לִהְיוֹת:


שדבר הכתוב בהווה – a thing that regularly happens.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:1:1)


**הַכּוֹנֵס.** פָּטוּר. דְּהָא נְטָרָהּ וּמַאי הָוֵי לֵיהּ לְמֶעֱבַד:


הכונס. פטור – for he guarded it and what could he have done?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:1:2)


**הוֹצִיאוּהָ לִסְטִים.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא הוֹצִיאוּהָ מַמָּשׁ אֶלָּא שֶׁעָמְדוּ בְּפָנֶיהָ עַד שֶׁיָּצְאָה, הָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ הוֹצִיאוּהָ בְּיָדַיִם וְחַיָּבִים:


הוציאוה לסטים – even though he did not actually take it out, but rather that they stood before it until she left – it is as if they removed it with their hands and they are liable.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:1)


**הִנִּיחָהּ בַּחַמָּה.** מְצַעֲרָה לַהּ שִׁמְשָׁא וְלֹא סַגִּי לַהּ בִּנְעִילָה כָּרָאוּי שֶׁהִיא בְּדֶלֶת שֶׁיְּכוֹלָה לַעֲמֹד בְּרוּחַ מְצוּיָה בִּלְבַד:


הניחה בחמה – the sun hurts her and it is not sufficient for her closing properly for it is in a door where she is able to stand only in an ordinary wind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:2)


**נִכְנָס הָרוֹעֶה תַּחְתָּיו.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן בִּכְהַאי גַּוְנָא שׁוֹמֵר שֶׁמָּסַר לְשׁוֹמֵר חַיָּב, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הָרוֹעֶה הַגָּדוֹל לִמְסֹר לָרוֹעֶה הַקָּטָן שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו, וּלְפִיכָךְ הָרוֹעֶה שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו חַיָּב:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:3)


**נָפְלָה לְגִנָּה.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהֻחְלְקָה וְנָפְלָה בְּאֹנֶס. אֲבָל דָּחוּ אוֹתָהּ חַבְרוֹתֶיהָ וְהִפִּילוּהָ, מְשַׁלֵּם מַה שֶּׁהִזִּיקָה, דְּאִיהוּ פָּשַׁע בָּהּ, דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ לַעֲבְרִינְהוּ חֲדָא חֲדָא:


נפלה לגינה – as in the case when she slipped and fell in by accident. But if her “friends” pushed her and caused her to abort [her fetus] he (i.e., the owner) pays what she damaged for she was negligent in it and he should have passed through one at a time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:4)


**מַה שֶּׁנֶּהֱנֵית.** לְפִי הֲנָאָתָהּ וְלֹא לְפִי הֶזֵּקָהּ:


מה שנהנית – according to her pleasure and not according to her damage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:5)


**שָׁמִין בֵּית סְאָה בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה.** אֵין שָׁמִין אֶת הָעֲרוּגָה לְבַדָּהּ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּפְסִיד מַזִּיק, שֶׁשָּׁמִין אוֹתָהּ בְּכָל שָׁוְיָהּ, וְרַחְמָנָא אָמַר וּבִעֵר בִּשְׂדֵה אַחֵר, וְדָרְשִׁינַן מְלַמֵּד שֶׁשָּׁמִין אוֹתָהּ עַל גַּב שָׂדֶה אַחֶרֶת. אֶלָּא שֶׁשָּׁמִין בֵּית סְאָה בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה כַּמָּה הָיְתָה שָׁוָה קֹדֶם שֶׁנֶּאֶכְלָה מִמֶּנָּה הָעֲרוּגָה הַזֹּאת, וְכַמָּה הִיא שָׁוָה עַכְשָׁיו, וְהַשְׁתָּא לֹא מְשַׁלֵּם כֻּלְּהוּ דָּמֶיהָ, דְּמִי שֶׁלּוֹקֵחַ בֵּית סְאָה כְּשֶׁהִיא בִּתְבוּאָתָהּ אֵינוֹ מְזַלְזֵל בָּהּ בִּשְׁבִיל הֶפְסֵד עֲרוּגָה אַחַת כִּי אִם דָּבָר מֻעָט:


שמין בית סאה באותה שדה – we do not estimate the garden bed alone because the damage causes loss when we estimate it according to its value, and the All-Merciful stated (Exodus 22:4): “[When a man lets his livestock loose] to graze in another’s land,” and we expound that it teaches that we estimate it on top of another field, but we estimate a Bet-Seah ( 2500 square cubits or 50 cubits square) in that field how much it was worth before the garden bed was eaten from how much it is worth now. But now, he does not pay all of its monetary value, for whomever purchases a Bet Seah when it is with its grain does not despise it for the loss of one garden bed for it is a small amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:2:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אָכְלָה פֵּרוֹת גְּמוּרִים.** שֶׁכְּבָר בָּשְׁלוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן, מְשַׁלֶּמֶת כָּל הַהֶזֵּק. וְהֵיכָא שָׁיְמִינַן אַגַּב שָׂדֶה, הֵיכָא דַּעֲדַיִן לֹא נִגְמְרוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון אומר אכלה פירות גמורים – for already they ripened fully – she (i.e., the owner of the animal) pays all the damages. But where we estimate on account of a field where it had not yet fully where it had not fully ripened. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:3:1)


**וְאִם הִגְדִּישׁ בִּרְשׁוּת בַּעַל הַשָּׂדֶה חַיָּב.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ בְּבִקְעָה, שֶׁרְגִילִין כֻּלָּן לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּגֹרֶן אֶחָד, זֶה גְּדִישׁוֹ וְזֶה גְּדִישׁוֹ, וּמְמַנִּין שׁוֹמֵר. וְכֵיוָן שֶׁאָמַר הַשּׁוֹמֵר עַיֵּל וּגְדֹשׁ, הָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ אָמַר עַיֵּל וְאֶנְטַר לָךְ. אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר בְּנֵי אָדָם, אֲפִלּוּ הִגְדִּישׁ בִּרְשׁוּת אֵין בַּעַל הַשָּׂדֶה חַיָּב, עַד דִּמְקַבֵּל עָלֶיהָ נְטִירוּתָא:


ואם הדגיש ברשות בעל השדה חייב – In the Gemara (Tractate Bava Kamma 59b), it establishes it in a valley, for everyone together are accustomed to make it in one garden bed. This is his heap of sheaves and that is the other’s heap of sheaves and they appoint a guard. And since the guard state that he will go up and make a heap of sheaves, it is as if he said: “I will go up and guard yours.” But with the rest of the people, even if he made a heap of sheaves with permission, the owner of the field is not liable until he accepts upon himself protection/guardianship.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:1)


**בָּא אַחֵר וְלִבָּה.** נָפַח בָּאֵשׁ וְהֶעֱלָה שַׁלְהֶבֶת, כְּמוֹ בְּלַבַּת אֵשׁ (שמות ג). וְאִית סְפָרִים דְּגָרְסֵי נִבָּה, מִגִּזְרַת נִיב שְׂפָתַיִם (ישעיה נז), כְּשֶׁאָדָם מְדַבֵּר מְנַעֲנֵעַ בִּשְׂפָתָיו וְרוּחַ יוֹצֵא:


בא אחר וליבה – he blew on the fire and it rose into a large flame like (Exodus 3:2): “[An angel of the LORD appeared to him] in a blazing fire” and there are those who have the reading "נבה" – from the decree (Isaiah 57:19): “heartening comforting words: [ it shall be well, Well with the far and the near – said the LORD-and I will heal them].” When a person speaks and moves his lips, and wind escapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:2)


**אוֹ עָפָר.** שֶׁלִּחֲכָה נִירוֹ וְנִתְקַלְקֵל:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:3)


**אוֹ דֶּרֶךְ הָרַבִּים.** שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, כְּדִגְלֵי הַמִּדְבָּר:


או דרך הרבים – sixteen cubits like the banners [of the tribes] in the wilderness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:4:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר הַכֹּל לְפִי הַדְּלֵקָה.** לְפִי גֹּבַהּ הַדְּלֵקָה וְגֹדֶל שִׁעוּרָהּ. שֶׁכְּשֶׁהָאֵשׁ גְּדוֹלָה קוֹפֶצֶת לְמֵרָחוֹק. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר הכל לפי הדליקה – according to the height of the fire and the size of the barley when the large fire jumps from afar. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:1)


**הַמַּדְלִיק אֶת הַגָּדִישׁ.** שֶׁהִדְלִיק בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ וְהָלְכָה וְאָכְלָה בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ:


המדליק את הגדיש – he kindled it within his own [property] and it went and consumed that of his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר יְשַׁלֵּם כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ.** דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְחַיֵּב עַל נִזְקֵי טָמוּן בָּאֵשׁ, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ דְּרָשָׁא דְּאוֹ הַקָּמָה מַה קָּמָה גְּלוּיָה אַף כָּל גָּלוּי:


ר' יהודה אומר ישלם כל מה שבתוכו – that Rabbi Yehuda obligates on hidden damage in the fire, for he does not have (i.e., hold) the expounded teaching on "או הקמה"/or the standing grain (see Exodus 22:5). Just as the standing grain is uncovered, even all that is uncovered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא גָּדִישׁ וְכוּ'.** דְּאִית לְהוּ דְּרָשָׁא דְּאוֹ הַקָּמָה, וּפָטְרֵי עַל נִזְקֵי טָמוּן בָּאֵשׁ. אֶלָּא שֶׁמְּשַׁעֲרִין מְקוֹם הַכֵּלִים כְּאִלּוּ הוּא גָּדִישׁ, וּמְשַׁלֵּם גָּדִישׁ כְּשִׁעוּר גּוּפָן שֶׁל כֵּלִים. וּמִדְּקָאָמְרֵי סֵיפָא וּמוֹדִים חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בְּמַדְלִיק אֶת הַבִּירָה שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁלֵּם כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, מוּכָח בַּגְּמָרָא שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ רַבִּי יְהוּדָה וַחֲכָמִים גַּם בְּמַדְלִיק בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, שֶׁרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר כְּשֶׁהִדְלִיק בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ וַאֲפִלּוּ אַרְנָקִי. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי כֵּלִים שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהַטְמִין בַּגָּדִישׁ כְּגוֹן מוֹרִיגִין וּכְלֵי בָּקָר הוּא דִּמְשַׁלֵּם, כֵּלִים שֶׁאֵין דַּרְכָּן לְהַטְמִין בַּגָּדִישׁ לֹא מְשַׁלֵּם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א אינו משלם אלא גדיש וכו' - they have (i.e., “hold by” this expounded teaching of "או הקמה" and exempt on all damages that are hidden in the fire, but they estimate the place of the utensils as if it is a heap of grain. And he pays the heap of grain like the measure of the body of the utensils and when we stated the end [of our Mishnah]: “And the Sages agree with Rabbi Yehuda” – when he sets fire to a large building, that he pays for all that is inside it. And it is proven in the Gemara (Tractate Bava Kamma 61b) that Rabbi Yehuda and the Rabbis disagreed also with one who kindles a fire in [the property] of his fellow, as Rabbi Yehuda holds that if he lights a fire in that of his fellow, he pays for everything that is within it, and even for a money-bag (or a purse hanging from the neck). But the Rabbis hold that utensils that one ordinarily hides in a heap of sheaves such as threshing sledges (i.e., an implement with grooves and indentations) and the utensils of cattle he pays for; utensils that one customarily does not hid in a heap of sheaves, he does not pay. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:4)


**הָיָה גְּדִי כָּפוּת לוֹ חַיָּב.** דְּבַעֲלֵי חַיִּים נַמִּי אִתְרַבּוּ מֵאוֹ הַקָּמָה. וּמִשּׁוּם דְּקָם לֵיהּ בִּדְרַבָּה מִנֵּיהּ לֵיכָּא לְמִפְטְרֵיהּ, דְּאֵינוֹ חַיָּב מִיתָה עַל הָעֶבֶד, הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ כָּפוּת הָיָה לוֹ לִבְרֹחַ, וּפָטוּר עָלָיו מִמִּיתָה וּמִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה עֶבֶד כָּפוּת לוֹ, פָּטוּר אֲפִלּוּ עַל הַגְּדִי וְעַל הַגָּדִישׁ, דְּחַיָּב מִיתָה עַל הָעֶבֶד וְקָם לֵיהּ בִּדְרַבָּה מִנֵּיהּ. וּבִגְדִי לֹא שָׁנֵי לָן בֵּין כָּפוּת לְאֵינוֹ כָּפוּת. וְאַיְדֵי דְּנָקַט בְּעֶבֶד נָקַט בִּגְדִי:


היה גדי כפות לו חייב – for living creatures are also included in "או הקמה"/or standing grain. And because of the case that he who has committed two offenses simultaneously, must be held answerable for the severer only, he cannot be exempted. That he is not liable for death on the slave, for since he was not tied up, he could have fled and would exempt about him from the death penalty and from payments. But if he was a slave tied up, he would be exempt even on the kid and on the heap of sheaves, but he would liable for the death penalty on the slave – for he who has committed two offenses simultaneously, must be held answerable for the severer only. But concerning the kid, it makes ono difference whether it was tied up or not tied up, for since it (i.e. the Mishnah) took the language of the slave, it took the language of the kid as well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:5:5)


**בְּמַדְלִיק אֶת הַבִּירָה.** דְּמַדְלִיק בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ הוּא, וְהָוֵי כִּמְאַבֵּד בַּיָּדַיִם. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי טַעֲמָא מִשּׁוּם דְּדֶרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְהַנִּיחַ כֵּלִים בַּבָּתִּים. אֲבָל בְּגָדִישׁ דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְהַנִּיחַ אֶלָּא כְּגוֹן מוֹרִיגִין וּכְלֵי בָּקָר, אַף עַל גַּב דְּאַדְלִיק לְתוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם אַלִּיבָּא דַּחֲכָמִים אֶלָּא דְּבָרִים שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהַטְמִין בַּגָּדִישׁ:


במדליק את הבירה – when he kindles within that of his fellow and it is as if he destroys with his hands, and even so, the reason because it is the manner of people to leave their utensils in their houses, but a heap of sheaves, where it is not the manner of people to leave it other than like a threshing sledge (i.e., an implement with grooves or indentations) or the instruments for cattle. Even though I will kindle with that of my fellow, he doesn’t pay, according to the Sages other than things that it is customary to hide in a heap of sheaves.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:6:1)


**גֵּץ.** נִיצוֹץ שֶׁל אֵשׁ:


גץ – a spark of fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/6:6:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בְּנֵר חֲנֻכָּה פָּטוּר.** כֵּיוָן דִּבְמִצְוָה קָא עָסֵיק. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר בנר חנוכה פטור – since he is engage in the performance of a Mitzvah. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:1:1)


**מְרֻבָּה.** בְּדָבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים וּבְדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים. דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) עַל שֶׂה עַל שַׂלְמָה עַל כָּל אֲבֵדָה וְגוֹ'‏ יְשַׁלֵּם שְׁנַיִם:


מרובה. דבר שיש בו רוח חיים ובדבר שאין בו רוח חיים – as it is written (Exodus 22:8): “[In all charges of misappropriation – pertaining to an ox, an ass,] a sheep, a garment, or any other loss, [whereof one party alleges, ‘This is it’ – the case of both parties shall come before God: he whom God declares guilty] shall pay double to the other.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:1:2)


**אֵין הַגּוֹנֵב אַחַר הַגַּנָּב וְכוּ'.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְגֻנַּב מִבֵּית הָאִישׁ, וְלֹא מִבֵּית הַגַּנָּב:


אין הגונב אחר הגנב – as it is written (Exodus 22:6): “and they are stolen from the man’s house,” and not “from the house of the thief.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:1)


**גָּנַב עַל פִּי שְׁנַיִם.** כְּלוֹמַר שְׁנַיִם מְעִידִין אוֹתוֹ שֶׁגָּנַב:


גנב על פי שנים – meaning to say that two testify against him that he stole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:2)


**גָּנַב וּמָכַר בְּשַׁבָּת.** אֲבָל טָבַח שֶׁהוּא עָנוּשׁ סְקִילָה, קָם לֵיהּ בִּדְרַבָּה מִנֵּיהּ:


גנב ומכר בשבת – but he slaughtered which is punishable by stoning, he who has committed two offenses simultaneously, must be held answerable for the severer only.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:3)


**וְטָבַח בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים.** שֶׁאֵין זְדוֹנוֹ אֶלָּא כָּרֵת. וּכְגוֹן דְּלֹא אַתְּרוּ בֵּיהּ וְלֹא לָקֵי, דְּקָיְמָא לָן כָּל חַיָּבֵי מַלְקִיּוֹת שׁוֹגְגִים חַיָּבִים בְּתַשְׁלוּמִין. אֲבָל חַיָּבֵי מִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין, אֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹגְגִים, פְּטוּרִים מִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין:


וטבח ביום הכפורים – for his wanton violation is not punishable other than through extirpation, and such as the case where they (i.e., the witnesses) didn’t warn him and they ae not whipped for we hold that all who are liable for flogging are those who act inadvertently, but are liable for payment, but those who are liable for the death penalty at the hands of the Jewish court , even inadvertently, are exempt from payment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:4)


**וְטָבַח וּמָכַר וְאַחַר כָּךְ מֵת אָבִיו.** אֲבָל מֵת אָבִיו וְאַחַר כָּךְ טָבַח, תָּנָא סֵיפָא דְּפָטוּר, דְּשֶׁלּוֹ הוּא טוֹבֵחַ וְשֶׁלּוֹ הוּא מוֹכֵר, שֶׁהֲרֵי יָרַשׁ אֶת אָבִיו:


וטבח ומכר ואחר כך מת אביו – but if his father died and afterwards, he slaughtered [the animal], it (i.e., the Mishnah] taught at the conclusion that he is exempt, because he is slaughtering what is his and selling what is his, for he inherited his father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:2:5)


**בִּשְׁנֵי אֵלּוּ.** בִּטְרֵפָה וּבְחֻלִּין בָּעֲזָרָה. דְּקָסָבַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לָאו שְׁמָהּ שְׁחִיטָה. אֲבָל לִרְפוּאָה וְלִכְלָבִים, שְׁחִיטָה רְאוּיָה הִיא, דְּאִי בָּעֵי מָצֵי אָכֵיל מִנַּהּ:


בשני אלו – with an animal torn by wild beasts and with non-holy produce eaten in the Temple courtyard, for Rabbi Shimon holds that slaughter that is not worthy is not called slaughtering, but for medicinal purposes and for dogs, it is an appropriate slaughter, and if he wants he can eat from it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:1)


**הָאַחֲרוֹנִים מְשַׁלְּמִים שְׁלֹשָׁה.** לְשׁוֹר. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהוּזְמוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים תְּחִלָּה. דְּאִי עֵדֵי גְּנֵבָה הוּזְמוּ תְּחִלָּה, בָּטְלָה לָהּ עֵדוּת טְבִיחָה, דְּדִלְמָא בְּעָלִים מְכָרוּהוּ לוֹ, וְכִי מִתַּזְּמֵי אַמַּאי מְשַׁלְּמֵי:


האחרונים משלמים שלשה – for an ox, and for example, the latter witnesses were found to be scheming witnesses first ,for if the witnesses for the theft had been found to be scheming first, the testimony of the slaughter is voided , for perhaps the owners had sold it to him, and if they are refuted why do they pay?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:2)


**בָּטְלָה עֵדוּת שְׁנִיָּה.** וְהוּא מְשַׁלֵּם כֶּפֶל מִשּׁוּם עֵדוּת רִאשׁוֹנִים. וְהֵן פְּטוּרִין, דְּאֵין עֵדִים מְשַׁלְּמִין מָמוֹן עַד שֶׁיּוּזְמוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם:


בטלה עדות שניה – and he pays double because of the first testimony (i.e., that he is a thief), and they are exempt, for the witnesses do not pay money until both sets are found to be scheming witnesses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:3:3)


**בָּטְלָה כָּל הָעֵדוּת.** וְהוּא פָּטוּר וְהֵם פְּטוּרִים. וַאֲפִלּוּ חָזְרוּ וְהוּזְמוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים אַחַר כָּךְ, אֵין מְשַׁלְּמִין, שֶׁהֲרֵי בָּטְלָה עֵדוּתָן כְּבָר וְהֻכְחֲשׁוּ, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא גָּנַב לֹא טָבַח, וְאַהָכִי לֹא מִחַיְּבוּ אֶלָּא אַעִמָּנוּ הֱיִיתֶם שֶׁהוּסְרָה גּוּפָה שֶׁל עֵדוּת. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם הוּזְמוּ שְׁנַיִם הָרִאשׁוֹנִים תְּחִלָּה, שֶׁעֵדוּת שְׁנִיָּה בָּטְלָה. אֶלָּא שֶׁבִּזְמַן שֶׁלֹּא הוּזְמוּ אֶלָּא אֶחָד, בָּטְלוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהוּזְמוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם, לֹא בָּטְלָה עֵדוּת רִאשׁוֹנָה אֶלָּא מְשַׁלְּמִין כֶּפֶל:


בטלה כל העדות – and he is exempt and they are exempt [from payment] and even if they retracted and the latter witnesses were found to be scheming afterwards, they do not pay as their testimony was voided already and they were contradicted. Since he did not steal, he did not slaughter [in the meanwhile] they are not liable. But regarding “you were with us” that the whole body of their testimony was removed and all the more so, if the first wo witnesses were found to be scheming witnesses, the testimony of the second [set of witnesses] is voided. But at the time that they had not been found to be scheming other than one [of them], the testimony of both is voided but when both of them had been proven to be scheming witnesses, the testimony of the first set, other than paying of double indemnity.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:1)


**עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּמִלְּתָא דִּפְשִׁיטָא הִיא דְּאֵין מְשַׁלֵּם אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד, הָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּעַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ דּוּמְיָא דְּעַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד, מָה עֵד אֶחָד אִי אָתֵי עֵד אֶחָד בָּתַר הָכִי מִצְטָרֵף בַּהֲדֵיהּ וּמִחַיַּב, עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ נַמִּי אִי אָתוּ עֵדִים בָּתַר הוֹדָאָתוֹ מְחַיְּבֵי לֵיהּ, דְּמוֹדֶה בִּקְנָס וְאַחַר כָּךְ בָּאוּ עֵדִים חַיָּב. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּאוֹמֵר לֹא גָּנַבְתִּי, וּבָאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁגָּנַב, וְחָזַר וְאָמַר טָבַחְתִּי וּמָכַרְתִּי, וּבָאוּ עֵדִים אַחַר כָּךְ שֶׁטָּבַח וּמָכַר, חַיָּב, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּשֶׁאוֹמֵר טָבַחְתִּי וּמָכַרְתִּי אֵינוֹ מְחַיֵּב עַצְמוֹ בִּכְלוּם, שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ הוּא שֶׁמּוֹדֶה בִּקְנָס פָּטוּר, וְאֵין כָּאן הוֹדָאָה שֶׁל כְּלוּם. אֲבָל בְּאָמַר גָּנַבְתִּי וּבָאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁגָּנַב, פָּטוּר. דְּחִיֵּב עַצְמוֹ לְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן מִיהָא בְּהוֹדָאָתוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ הוֹדָאָה גְּמוּרָה הִיא וּפָטוּר מִכֶּפֶל, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאַחַר כָּךְ בָּאוּ עֵדִים:


על פי עד אחד – even though it is a simple mater, for one does not pay four or five times the amount (i.e., if he not only stole, but also sold the animal or slaughtered it – see Exodus 21:37) on the testimony of one witness. Sure this comes to teach us that testifying by one’s own admission is similar to testimony by one witness. Just as one witnesses, if he brings another witness after wars, they combine in public to make liable. By his own testimony also, if he brings witnesses after his admission, they make him liable, for admitting to a fine and afterwards witnesses came, he is liable. And these words [concern] someone who says: “ I didn’t steal,” and witnesses came and said that he stole and afterwards he retracted and said: “I slaughtered or I sold,” and witnesses came afterwards that he slaughtered or sold, he is liable, for when he says, “I slaughtered or I sold,” he does not make himself liable for anything for he knows that a person who admits to a fine is exempt. We we don’t have here an admission of nothing. But when he says: “I sole” and witnesses came [and testified} that he stole, he obligated himself to pay the principle, however, through his admission, therefore, it is a complete admission and he is exempt from double indemnity, even though afterwards witnesses came [and testified].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:2)


**וּמֵת אָבִיו.** וְהוּא יוֹרְשׁוֹ, וְלֹא הַוְיָא טְבִיחָה כֻּלָּהּ בְּאִסּוּרָא:


ומת אביו – and he inherited him and would not be a slaughter completely illicit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:3)


**גָּנַב וְהִקְדִּישׁ.** כִּי קָא טָבַח דְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ קָא טָבַח וְלֹא דִּבְעָלִים:


גנב והקדיש – when he slaughtered it was for the Temple that he slaughtered and not for the owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:4:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר קָדָשִׁים שֶׁחַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָם וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לָאו אַמִּלְּתֵיהּ דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא קָאֵי וְלֹא פָּלֵיג עֲלֵיהּ בְּגָנַב וְהִקְדִּישׁ וְאַחַר כָּךְ טָבַח וּמָכַר. אֶלָּא שַׁמְעִינְהוּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לְרַבָּנָן בְּדוּכְתָּא אַחֲרִיתִי דְּאָמְרֵי הַגּוֹנֵב הֶקְדֵּשׁ מִבֵּית בְּעָלִים פָּטוּר, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) וְגֻנַּב מִבֵּית הָאִישׁ, וְלֹא מִבֵּית הֶקְדֵּשׁ, וַעֲלַהּ קָאֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן וְאוֹמֵר קָדָשִׁים שֶׁחַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן חַיָּב, דְּקָרִינַן בֵּיהּ וְגֻנַּב מִבֵּית הָאִישׁ, דְּכֵיוָן דְּהַבַּעַל חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָם כִּי קָא טָבַח דְּמָרֵיהּ קָא טָבַח. וּבְקָדָשִׁים שֶׁחַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן נַמִּי לֹא מְחַיֵּב רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן תַּשְׁלוּמֵי אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁשְּׁחָטָן תְּמִימִים בִּפְנִים לְשֵׁם בְּעָלִים אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּשְׁפַּךְ הַדָּם, אוֹ שֶׁשְּׁחָטָן בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין בַּחוּץ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִפְדּוּ, סָבַר כָּל הָעוֹמֵד לִפָּדוֹת כְּפָדוּי דָּמֵי וְהַוְיָא שְׁחִיטָה רְאוּיָה. אֲבָל אִם שְׁחָטָן תְּמִימִים בַּחוּץ הַוְיָא שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה. וְשָׁמְעִינַן לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמַר שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לָאו שְׁמָהּ שְׁחִיטָה וְאֵינוֹ חַיָּב עָלֶיהָ תַּשְׁלוּמֵי אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר קדשים שחייב באחריותם (See Tractate Megillah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6 and Tracate Kinnim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 – both at the end of the respective Mishnayot.) Rabbi Shimon is not referring to the matter of the first Tanna/teacher, nor does he dispute him regarding someone who stole and dedicated the animal to the Temple and afterwards slaughtered and/or sold it. But we understand Rabbi Shimon teaching the Rabbis in another place and saying that a person who seals something dedicated to the Temple from the house of its owners is exempt, as it is written (Exodus 22:6): “if they are stolen from the man’s house” and not “from the house of something dedicated to the Temple.” And on this, Rabbi Shimon refers and states “that a person if he stole consecrated animals for which he [who had consecrated them] is responsible is liable,” for we call him, “if they are stolen from the man’s house”, and since the owner is responsible for them, he is liable. But when he slaughters, it the master’s that he slaughters, and in consecrated animals where he is responsible for the, Rabbi Shimon also does not obligate him for the payment of four times (for a sheep) the amount or five times (for an ox) the amount other than when he slaughtered them innocently inside for the sake of the owners, but when the blood is spilled, if those who have bodily defects slaughtered them outside, even though they had not been redeemed, he held that anything about to be redeemed is considered as fully redeemed. And it is an appropriate slaughter, but if pure people had slaughtered them outside [the Temple] it is considered an unworthy slaughtering, and we understand that according to Rabbi Shimon who said that a slaughter that is not fit is not called a slaughtering, and he is not liable for the payment of four and five times the amount either. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:1)


**מְכָרוֹ חוּץ מֵאֶחָד מִמֵּאָה שֶׁבּוֹ.** גַּנָּב שֶׁמָּכַר כָּל הַשּׁוֹר חוּץ מִדָּבָר מֻעָט שֶׁבּוֹ, מִן הַדְּבָרִים הַנִּתָּרִים עִמּוֹ בַּשְּׁחִיטָה, פָּטוּר, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וּטְבָחוֹ אוֹ מְכָרוֹ, עַד שֶׁיִּמְכֹּר כָּל הַדְּבָרִים הַנִּתָּרִים בַּטְּבִיחָה. לַאֲפוֹקֵי שֶׁאִם הִשְׁאִיר גִּזּוֹתֶיהָ אוֹ קַרְנֶיהָ, דְּלֹא הָוֵי שִׁיּוּר, וְלֹא מִפְטַר בְּהָכִי:


מכרו חוץ מאחד ממאה שבו – a thief who sold the entire ox except for a small amount of it, from the things that remain with him in the slaughter he is exempt, as it is written (Exodus 21:37): “[When a man steals an ox or a sheep,] and slaughters it or sells it “ -until he sells all of the things that remain from the slaughter, excluding if some of its shorn wool/fleece, etc. and he is not exempted from this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:2)


**הַנּוֹחֵר.** קוֹרְעוֹ מִנְּחִירָיו עַד לִבּוֹ:


הנוחר – tears it open from its nostrils until its heart.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:5:3)


**וְהַמְעַקֵּר.** עוֹקֵר סִימָנִים, פָּטוּר. וַאֲפִלּוּ לְרַבָּנָן דְּאָמְרֵי שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה שְׁמָהּ שְׁחִיטָה, דְּהָא לָאו שְׁחִיטָה הִיא כְּלָל:


והמעקר – uproots the windpipe and gullet is exempt, and even according to the Rabbis who state that a ritual slaughtering ha is not appropriate is called a ritual slaughtering, but this is not a ritual slaughtering at all.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:1)


**הָיָה מוֹשְׁכוֹ וְיוֹצֵא.** פָּטוּר מִכֶּפֶל:


היה מושכו ויוצא – he is exempt from the twofold restitution.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:2)


**הִגְבִּיהוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת בְּעָלִים. שֶׁהֲרֵי הַגְבָּהָה קוֹנָה בְּכָל מָקוֹם:


הגביהו – even in the domain of its owners for lifting up acquires in every place (see also, Tractate Kiddushin, Chapter 1, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:3)


**נְתָנוֹ.** גַּנָּב לַכֹּהֵן, בְּחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים שֶׁל פִּדְיוֹן בְּנוֹ:


נתנו – he stole it for a Kohen for five Selah of the Redemption of his First-Born son.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:4)


**הָיָה מוֹשְׁכוֹ.** הַכֹּהֵן אוֹ בַּעַל חוֹב אוֹ הַשּׁוֹמֵר. וּמֵת בִּרְשׁוּת בְּעָלִים:


היה מושכו – the Kohen or the creditor or the guard and it died in the domain of the owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:6:5)


**פָּטוּר.** הַגַּנָּב מִכְּלוּם:


פטור – the thief [is exempt] from none of it.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:1)


**אֵין מְגַדְּלִין בְּהֵמָה דַּקָּה בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִשּׁוּם יִשּׁוּב אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁמַּפְסִידִין אֶת הַזְּרָעִים:


אין מגדלין בהמה דקה בארץ ישראל – because the settling of the land of Israel which causes the loss of the seeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:2)


**אֲבָל מְגַדְּלִין בְּסוּרְיָא.** אֲרָצוֹת שֶׁכָּבַשׁ דָּוִד. דְּלָאו שְׁמֵיהּ כִּבּוּשׁ, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן בָּהּ לְיִשּׁוּב. וְאִם יַפְסִידוּ שְׂדוֹת אֲחֵרִים, יְשַׁלְּמֶנָּה:


אבל מגדלין בסוריא – lands that [King] David conquered but are not called conquest, and we don’t take into consideration of it for settlement, but if they causes loss to other fields, he has to pay it back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:3)


**אֵין מְגַדְּלִין תַּרְנְגוֹלִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם מִפְּנֵי הַקָּדָשִׁים.** שֶׁאוֹכְלִין שָׁם. וְדֶרֶךְ תַּרְנְגוֹלִים לְנַקֵּר בָּאַשְׁפָּה, וְשֶׁמָּא יָבִיאוּ עֶצֶם [כִּשְׂעֹרָה] מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ וִיטַמְּאוּ אֶת הַקָּדָשִׁים:


אין מגדלין תרנגולין בירושלים מפני קדשים – for they eat there, and it is the manner of chickens to gnaw at the trash, and perhaps would bring a bone [like the size of a piece of barley] from the unclean reptile and would ritually defile that holy things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:4)


**וְלֹא.** יְגַדְּלוּ כֹּהֲנִים תַּרְנְגוֹלִים בְּכָל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ולא – Kohanim should not raise chickens throughout all of the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:5)


**מִפְּנֵי הַטָּהֳרוֹת.** שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים אוֹכְלִים תְּרוּמָה וּצְרִיכִים לְשָׁמְרָהּ בְּטָהֳרָה:


מפני הטהרות – for he Kohanim eat priest’s due and need to preserve it in ritual purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:6)


**חֲזִירִים.** מְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא בַּגְּמָרָא, כְּשֶׁצָּרוּ מַלְכֵי חַשְׁמוֹנַאי זֶה עַל זֶה הָיוּ רְגִילִים בְּכָל יוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ אֵלּוּ שֶׁבַּחוּץ מַעֲלִים לָהֶם תְּמִידִים לְאֵלּוּ שֶׁבִּפְנִים. יוֹם אֶחָד הֶעֱלוּ לָהֶם חֲזִיר, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לַחֲצִי חוֹמָה נָעַץ צִפָּרְנָיו בַּחוֹמָה וְנִזְדַּעְזְעָה אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה עַל אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה. בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה אָמְרוּ אָרוּר הַמְגַדֵּל חֲזִירִים:


חזירים – the reason is explained in the Gemara (Tractate Bava Kamma 81b) when the Kings of the Hasmoneans split with each other, it was customary each day that those who were outside would bring up to them daily [burnt] offerings to those who were inside. One day, they brought up to them a swine, and since it reached o the half-way point of the wall, the swine pressed its nails against the wall and all of the Land of Israel shook four hundred parasang by four hundred parasang. At that hour, they said: cursed be the tower of the swine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:7)


**אֶת הַכֶּלֶב.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנּוֹשֵׁךְ וּמְנַבֵּחַ וּמַפֶּלֶת אִשָּׁה מִיִּרְאָתוֹ:


את הכלב – because it bites and it barks and causes a woman to abort [her fetus] from her fear of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:8)


**נִשְׁבִּים.** פַּחִים. שֶׁלֹּא יִלָּכְדוּ בָּהֶם יוֹנֵי בְּנֵי הַיִּשּׁוּב:


נשבים – traps so that doves who are part of the settlement will not be seized.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 7:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/7:7:9)


**שְׁלֹשִׁים רִיס.** אַרְבָּעָה מִילִין:


שלשים ריס – four miles.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:1)


**הַחוֹבֵל.** וְכַמָּה הוּא יָפֶה. שֶׁאִם הָיָה צָרִיךְ הָיָה מוֹכֵר עַצְמוֹ בְּעֶבֶד עִבְרִי, וְזֶה שֶׁהִזִּיקוֹ הִפְסִידוֹ מָמוֹן זֶה:


החובל. וכמה הוא יפה – for if he needed to, he would sell himself as a Hebrew slave. But the one who damages him causes him the loss of this money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:2)
