**כַּיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה.** לְפִי מַה שֶּׁהוּא מְעֻנָּג רַב צַעֲרוֹ וּכְאֵבוֹ:


כיוצא בזה – according to what he enjoys, the greatness of his trouble and pain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:3)


**צְמָחִין.** אֲבַעְבּוּעוֹת לְבָנוֹת:


צמחין – white pustules.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:4)


**שֶׁבֶת.** כָּל יְמֵי הַחֹלִי רוֹאִין אוֹתוֹ כְּאִלּוּ הוּא שׁוֹמֵר קִשּׁוּאִין וְנוֹתֵן שְׂכִירוּתוֹ שֶׁל כָּל יוֹם, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין רָאוּי לִמְלָאכָה אַחֶרֶת אֲפִלּוּ בְּלֹא חֹלִי, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִקְטַע יָדוֹ וְרַגְלוֹ וְהוּא כְּבָר נָתַן לוֹ דְּמֵיהֶן:


שבת – all the days of the illness, we see him as if he is watchman of cucumbers and provide his salary of each day. For behold he is not fit for another labor even without illness for his hand is cut off or his leg and he has already been given their value.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:5)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי הַמְבַיֵּשׁ.** אָדָם קַל שֶׁבִּיֵּשׁ בָּשְׁתּוֹ מְרֻבָּה:


הכל לפי המבייש – an unimportant man who was put to shame, his insult is greater.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:1:6)


**וְהַמִּתְבַּיֵּשׁ.** לְפִי חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ בָּשְׁתּוֹ מְרֻבָּה. וְכֻלְּהוּ חֲמִשָּׁה דְּבָרִים מִקְּרָא נָפְקֵי. נֶזֶק, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) עַיִן תַּחַת עַיִן, וְאֵין לוֹמַר עַיִן מַמָּשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר לה) וְלֹא תִּקְּחוּ כֹּפֶר לְנֶפֶשׁ רוֹצֵחַ, לְנֶפֶשׁ רוֹצֵחַ אִי אַתָּה לוֹקֵחַ כֹּפֶר אֲבָל אַתָּה לוֹקֵחַ כֹּפֶר לְרָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, שֶׁאִם סִמֵּא אֶת עֵין חֲבֵרוֹ נוֹתֵן לוֹ דְּמֵי עֵינוֹ, וְהַיְנוּ עַיִן תַּחַת עַיִן. צַעַר, נָפְקָא לָן מִפֶּצַע תַּחַת פָּצַע (שמות כא), דִּקְרָא יְתֵרָא הוּא לְחַיֵּב עַל הַצַּעַר וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם נֶזֶק, דְּלָא תֵימָא הֲרֵי קָנָה יָדוֹ וְיֵשׁ עָלָיו לְחָתְכָהּ, אֶלָּא אוֹמְרִים הָיָה עָלָיו לְחָתְכָהּ בְּסַם וְזֶה חֲתָכָהּ בְּבַרְזֶל וְצִעֲרוֹ, לְפִיכָךְ מְשַׁלֵּם אֶת הַצַּעַר. רִפּוּי וְשֶׁבֶת, רַק שִׁבְתּוֹ יִתֵּן וְרַפֹּא יְרַפֵּא (שם). וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהַחֹלִי בָּא מֵחֲמַת מַכָּה. אֲבָל אִם פָּשַׁע הַחוֹלֶה בְּעַצְמוֹ וְעָבַר עַל דִּבְרֵי הָרוֹפֵא, שׁוּב אֵין הַמַּזִּיק חַיָּב בַּשֶּׁבֶת וְרִפּוּי שֶׁצְּרִיכִין מֵחֲמַת פְּשִׁיעוּתָא. בֹּשֶׁת, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) וְקַצֹּתָה אֶת כַּפָּהּ, מָמוֹן. וְדִין תּוֹרָה שֶׁאֵין יְכוֹלִים לָדוּן בְּשׁוּם דִּין שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם אֶלָּא דַּיָּנִים הַסְּמוּכִים בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) עַד הָאֱלֹהִים יָבֹא דְּבַר שְׁנֵיהֶם, וְאֵין נִקְרָאִים אֱלֹהִים אֶלָּא דַּיָּנִים הַסְּמוּכִים בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. אֶלָּא שֶׁבְּהַלְוָאוֹת וּמִקָּח וּמִמְכָּר וְהַקְנָאוֹת וְהוֹדָאוֹת וּכְפִירוֹת דָּנִין בָּהֶם בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ כְּאִלּוּ הֵם שְׁלוּחִים שֶׁל בֵּית דִּין שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּשְׁלִיחוּתַיְהוּ קָעָבְדִינַן. וְזֶה בִּלְבַד בְּדָבָר הַמָּצוּי וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ חֶסְרוֹן כִּיס, וְכֵן בְּהֵמָה שֶׁהִזִּיקָה בְּשֵׁן וָרֶגֶל שֶׁהֵם מוּעָדִים, אוֹ שֶׁהִזִּיק אָדָם בִּבְהֵמָה. אֲבָל בְּהֵמָה שֶׁהִזִּיקָה אָדָם, אוֹ אָדָם בְּאָדָם, אֵין דָּנִין אוֹתוֹ בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ כְּלָל, אֶלָּא מְנַדִּין אֶת הַחוֹבֵל אוֹ אֶת הַמַּזִּיק עַד שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה עִם בַּעַל דִּינוֹ לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹ שֶׁיִּתֵּן דֶּרֶךְ פְּשָׁרָה קָרוֹב לְמַה שֶּׁנִּרְאֶה בְּעֵינֵי הַדַּיָּן, אֲבָל דָּבָר קָצוּב אֵין פּוֹסְקִים עָלָיו. וְכֵן הַדִּין בְּכָל הַקְּנָסוֹת הַכְּתוּבוֹת בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְכֻלָּהּ תַּלְמוּדָא, אֵין גּוֹבִין אוֹתָן דַּיָּנֵי חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, אֶלָּא שֶׁמְּנַדִּין מִי שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב בָּהֶן כְּמוֹ שֶׁבֵּאַרְנוּ:


והמתבייש – according to his importance is his shame [greater]. And each of these five things are derived from Biblical verses. Damages/נזק, as it is written (Exodus 21:24): “eye for eye” – and that does not mean to say an actual eye, as it is written (Numbers 35:31): “You may not accept a ransom for the life of a murderer.” For the life of a murderer you don’t take a ransom, but you do take a ransom for the heads of his limbs, for if he blinded he eye of his fellow, we give him the value of the eye, and that is “eye for an eye.” Pain/צער – we derive from (Exodus 21:25): “wound for wound.”. For it is an additional verse making him liable for the pain and even in the place of damage. And you should not say, behold he acquired his hand, and he now must cut it off. But we say that he should have cut it off with a drug, and this one severed it with iron and caused him pain. Therefore, he pays for the pain [that he caused]. ריפוי ושבת /healing and idleness/sitting. (Exodus 21:19): “Except that he must pay for his idleness and his cure,” and specifically if the illness comes on account of the wound. But if the hill person was negligent regarding himself and transgressed the words of the physician, the one who did damage is not liable for idleness and healing for we require on account of negligence. בשת/insult or indignity – as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:12): “You shall cut off her hand; [show no pity] – it is money. And the law of the Torah is that one cannot judge aa single law in the world other than judges who are ordained in the Land of Israel, as it is written (Exodus 22:8): “the case of both parties shall come before God.” And they [who judge] are not called "אלהים"/God, other than those who are ordained in the Land of Israel. But loans, business transactions, agreements by which one’s landed estate is mortgaged in the form of a sale from date (independent of he loan to consummated afterwards so that at a certain date the creditor can claim the property, even if sold in the meantime, by referring to the priority of his purchase (i.e., deeds of transfer), admissions and denials, we adjudicate them outside the Land [of Israel] as if they (i.e., the judges) are representatives of the Jewish court of the Land of Israel and their agency we make use of. And this alone in a found matter where there is a [potential] loss of money [and similarly] an animal that damaged with the tooth or the foot and they are forewarned, or if a man caused damage to an animal. But an animal that caused damage to a person, or a person to another person, we don’t judge hem outside the Land [of Israel] at all. Rather, we excommunicate the person who wounds or the one who causes damage, until he go up with his fellow litigant to the Land of Israel or that he will provide, through the path of compromise, something close to what appears in the eyes of the judge. But a limited matter, they don’t render a decision upon. And the same law applies to fines that written in the Torah and in all of them is Talmudic learning: We don’t collect them – the judges of the Diaspora, but rather we excommunicate whomever is liable for them as we have explained.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:2:1)


**וְשׁוֹר אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא נֶזֶק.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כד) אִישׁ בַּעֲמִיתוֹ, וְלֹא שׁוֹר בַּעֲמִיתוֹ:


ושור אינו משלם אלא נזק – as it is written (Leviticus 24:19): “If anyone [maims] his fellow, [as he has done so shall it be done to him,” and not an ox for its fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:2:2)


**וּפָטוּר מִדְּמֵי וְלָדוֹת.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) כִּי יִנָּצוּ אֲנָשִׁים, אֲנָשִׁים וְלֹא שְׁוָרִים:


ופטור מדמי ולדות – as it is written (Exodus 21:22): “When men fight” - men and not oxen.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:3:1)


**וְהַחוֹבֵל בַּחֲבֵרוֹ בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים חַיָּב.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְכָל הַתּוֹרָה כֻּלָּהּ הָעוֹבֵר עֲבֵרָה שֶׁחַיָּב עָלֶיהָ מַלְקוֹת וְתַשְׁלוּמִין לוֹקֶה וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם, הָכָא מְשַׁלֵּם וְאֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה, דִּבְפֵרוּשׁ רִבְּתָה תּוֹרָה חוֹבֵל בַּחֲבֵרוֹ לְתַשְׁלוּמִין וְלֹא לְמַלְקוֹת, מִדִּכְתִיב (דברים יט) יָד בְּיָד, דְּהַיְנוּ מָמוֹן, מִכְּדִי כְּתִיב (ויקרא כד) כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה כֵן יֵעָשֶׂה לוֹ, יָד בְּיָד לָמָּה לִי, אֶלָּא לְהָבִיא חוֹבֵל בַּחֲבֵרוֹ בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים, שֶׁמְּשַׁלֵּם וְאֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה:


החובל בחבירו ביה"כ חייב – even though that in the entire Torah , a person who violates a transgression for which he is liable for flogging and payment, he is flogged but does not pay. Here, he pays and is not flogged. For in the commentary, the Torah widened the scope of the law concerning a person who strikes his fellow for payment and not for stripes, from what is written (Deuteronomy 19:21): “hand for hand,” which is money since it is written (Leviticus 24:19): “as he has done so shall it be done to him.” Why does it say “hand for hand?” But rather to include a person who strikes his fellow on Yom Kippur that pays and does not get flogged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:3:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֵין לָעֲבָדִים בֹּשֶׁת.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) כִּי יִנָּצוּ אֲנָשִׁים יַחְדָּו אִישׁ וְאָחִיו, בְּמִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ אַחֲוָה, יָצָא עֶבֶד שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אַחֲוָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר אין לעבדים בושת – as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:11): “If two men get into a fight with each other,” for whom that he has brotherhood, excluding a slave who lacks brotherhood. Ut the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:4:1)


**עֶבֶד וְאִשָּׁה שֶׁחָבְלוּ בַּאֲחֵרִים פְּטוּרִים.** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם בַּמֶּה לְשַׁלֵּם:


עבד ואשה שחבלו באחרים פטורים – for they lack what to pay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:4:2)


**נִתְגָּרְשָׁה הָאִשָּׁה וְנִשְׁתַּחְרֵר הָעֶבֶד.** וְקָנוּ נְכָסִים. חַיָּבִין לְשַׁלֵּם. שֶׁהֲרֵי מִתְּחִלָּה הֵן חַיָּבִין, אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם מַה לְּשַׁלֵּם. שֶׁנִּכְסֵי מְלוֹג שֶׁל אִשָּׁה מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לַבַּעַל לְפֵרוֹת וְלִירֻשָּׁה:


נתגרשה האשה ונשתחרר העבד – and they acquired property, they are liable to pay. For at the beginning, they are liable, but they lack what to pay as the usufruct of the wife is mortgaged to her husband for its fruits and for inheritance.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:5:1)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא נִדּוֹן בְּנַפְשׁוֹ.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מְקַלְקֵל, מְתַקֵּן הוּא אֵצֶל יִצְרוֹ, שֶׁשָּׁכְכָה חֲמָתוֹ וְנָח רָגְזוֹ בְּכָךְ:


מפני שהוא נדון בנפשו – for even though he is disgraced, he is repaired regarding his Creator, since his wrath has been appeased and his anger has been put to rest through [his act].



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:1)


**הַתּוֹקֵעַ לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** שֶׁמְּחַבֵּר אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו בְּפַס יָדוֹ וּמַכֶּה אוֹתוֹ בָּאֶגְרוֹף:


התוקע לחבירו – who attaches his fingers in the palm of his hand and strikes him with a fist (see Talmud Bava Kamma 90a about other possible meanings – like slapping his neighbor on the ear or shouting into his ear).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:2)


**נוֹתֵן לוֹ סֶלַע.** דְּמֵי בָּשְׁתּוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁאוֹמֵר מָנֶה:


נותן לו סלע – the monetary value of his embarrassment. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda who holds that he gives him a Maneh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:3)


**סְטָרוֹ.** שֶׁהִכָּהוּ בְּפַס יָדוֹ עַל לֶחְיוֹ, וְאִיכָּא בֹּשֶׁת טְפֵי:


סטרו – that he struck him with the palm of his hand on his cheek, and there is greater indignity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:4)


**צָרַם.** מָשַׁךְ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר, פָּגַם:


צרם – pulled. Another language: he cut/damaged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:5)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי כְּבוֹדוֹ.** כָּל אֵלּוּ הַדָּמִים שֶׁהֻזְכְּרוּ בַּמִּשְׁנָה אֵינָם אֶלָּא לִמְכֻבָּד בְּיוֹתֵר. אֲבָל לְאָדָם בָּזוּי פּוֹחֲתִין לוֹ:


הכל לפי כבודו – all of these monies that were mentioned in the Mishnah are not other than for the most honored. But for a despised person, we lessen it for him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:6)


**אָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא, וְסָבַר דְּבֵין מְכֻבָּד בֵּין בָּזוּי, כֻּלָּן שָׁוִין לְדִינֵי קְנָסוֹת הַלָּלוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


אמר רבי עקיבא וכו' – Rabbi Akiva disputes with the first Tanna/teacher as he holds that whether the person is honored or despised, they are equivalent for the laws of these fines. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:7)


**וְנָתַן לוֹ זְמָן.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי לְבֹשֶׁת, דְּלֹא חַסְּרֵיהּ מָמוֹנָא, יָהֲבִינַן זְמַן. אֲבָל לִנְזָקִין, דְּחַסְּרֵיהּ מָמוֹנָא, לֹא יָהֲבִינַן זְמַן:


ונתן לו זמן – and these words are for indignity when it doesn’t cause loss of money, we give him time. But for damages that cause him loss of money, we don’t give him time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:8)


**שְׁמָרָהּ.** הִמְתִּין לָהּ עַד שֶׁרָאָה אוֹתָהּ עוֹמֶדֶת עַל פֶּתַח חֲצֵרָהּ:


שימר – he waited until he saw here standing at the entrance of her courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:9)


**וּבוֹ כְּאִסָּר שֶׁמֶן.** שֶׁמֶן קָנוּי בְּאִסָּר:


ובו כאיסר שמן – oil that is purchased for an Issar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:6:10)


**לָזוֹ אֲנִי נוֹתֵן אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת זוּז.** שֶׁעַל כְּאִסָּר שֶׁמֶן זִלְזְלָה בְּעַצְמָהּ לְגַלּוֹת רֹאשָׁהּ וּמַרְאָה הִיא שֶׁאֵינָהּ מַקְפֶּדֶת עַל הַבֹּשֶׁת:


לזו אני נותן ארבע מאות זוז – for on an Issar’s worth of oil she disregarded herself to reveal her head and she shows that she is not strict about indignity/embarrassment.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/8:7:1)


**עַל מְנָת לִפְטֹר חַיָּב.** אִם שְׁאָלוֹ חוֹבֵל לַנֶּחְבָּל, עַל מְנָת לִפְטֹר אוֹתִי אַתָּה אוֹמֵר סַמֵּא אֶת עֵינִי, וְהֵשִׁיבוֹ הַנֶּחְבָּל הֵן, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן חַיָּב, שֶׁיֵּשׁ הֵן שֶׁהוּא כְּלָאו, וּבִלְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָא אָמַר לוֹ הֵן, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לִמְחֹל עַל צַעַר גּוּפָן. אֲבָל הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ שַׁבֵּר אֶת כַּדִּי, וּשְׁאָלוֹ הַמַּזִּיק עַל מְנָת לִפְטֹר אוֹתִי אַתָּה אוֹמֵר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֱשִׁיבוֹ נִזָּק לָאו, זֶה הַלָּאו הוּא כְּהֵן, וּכְאִלּוּ אָמַר לוֹ וְכִי לֹא אָמַרְתִּי לְךָ עַל מְנָת לִפְטֹר, וּלְפִיכָךְ פָּטוּר, שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לִמְחֹל עַל נִזְקֵי מָמוֹן:


על מנת לפטור חייב – if the one who wounds would ask the one wounded, on condition to exempt me , you say, “blind my eye,” and the wounded responded “yes,” for it is not the manner of human beings to pardon on the pain of their bodies, but if a person says to his fellow: “break my pitcher,” and the one doing damage asked: “on condition that you will exempt me you state this,” even though the one who suffered damage responded to him negatively, this “no” is like a “yes”, and it is as if he said to: “but didn’t I not say to you on condition that I would be exempt?” And therefore, he is exempt, for such is the manner of human beings to pardon on monetary damages.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:1)


**הַגּוֹזֵל.** מְשַׁלֵּם כִּשְׁעַת הַגְּזֵלָה. דְּמֵי עֵצִים וְצֶמֶר. וְאֵין חַיָּב לְהַחְזִיר לוֹ כֵּלִים, דְּקָנֵי בְּשִׁנּוּי:


הגוזל. משלם כשעת הגזילה – the cost of wood and/or wool and he is not liable to return to him utensils, for they were acquired through the change.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:2)


**דְּמֵי רָחֵל הָעוֹמֶדֶת לִגָּזֵז.** וְהָעֹדֶף שֶׁשָּׁוֶה יוֹתֵר הַוָּלָד וְהַגִּזָּה, שֶׁלּוֹ הוּא, דִּקְנַנְּהוּ בְּשִׁנּוּי:


דמי רחל העומדת ליגזז – and the surplus that is worth more than the offspring and the sheering [of wool]. It is his (i.e., belongs to him) as he acquired it through the change.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:1:3)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי גָּנַב טָלֶה וְנַעֲשָׂה אַיִל, עֵגֶל וְנַעֲשָׂה שׁוֹר, וּטְבָחוֹ אוֹ מְכָרוֹ, שֶׁהוּא פָּטוּר מִתַּשְׁלוּמֵי אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה שִׁנּוּי בְּיָדוֹ, קְנָאוֹ, וְשֶׁלּוֹ הוּא טוֹבֵחַ וְשֶׁלּוֹ הוּא מוֹכֵר:


זה הכלל – to include [someone] who stole a lamb and which grew to be a ram, a calf and it became an ox and he slaughtered it or sold it that he is exempt from paying four (sheep for the sheep) or five (oxen for the ox – see Exodus 21:37), for since a change had occurred while in his hand, he acquired it. For something that is his, he can slaughter or sell it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:1)


**בַּעֲבָדִים אוֹמֵר לוֹ הֲרֵי שֶׁלְּךָ לְפָנֶיךָ.** דִּכְקַרְקַע דָּמוּ וּבִרְשׁוּתָא דְּמָרַיְהוּ קַיְּמֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


בעבדים אומר לו הרי שלך לפניך – for they are like land and exist in the domain of their master. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:2)


**מַטְבֵּעַ וְנִסְדַּק.** שִׁנּוּי הַנִּכָּר הוּא:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:3)


**פֵּרוֹת וְהִרְקִיבוּ.** כְּשֶׁהִרְקִיבוּ מִקְצָתָן [אוֹמֵר לוֹ הֲרֵי שֶׁלְּךָ לְפָנֶיךָ ]. אֲבָל הִרְקִיבוּ כֻּלָּם, מְשַׁלֵּם כִּשְׁעַת הַגְּזֵלָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:4)


**מַטְבֵּעַ וְנִפְסַל.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹבֵר בְּאוֹתָהּ מְדִינָה, אֲבָל עוֹבֵר הוּא בִּמְדִינָה אַחֶרֶת:


מטבע ונפסל – it is not in circulation in that country, but it is in circulation in another country.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:5)


**וְנֶעֶבְדָה בָהּ עֲבֵרָה.** שֶׁנִּרְבְּעָה אוֹ נֶעֶבְדָה [לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה] שֶׁהִיא פְּסוּלָה לְקָרְבָּן:


ונעבדה בה עבירה – it was copulated in an unnatural way or was used for [idolatrous] purposes, it is ritually unfit for a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:2:6)


**שֶׁנִּפְסְלָה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** בְּמוּם שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִכָּר כְּגוֹן בְּדוֹקִין שֶׁבָּעַיִן:


שנפסלה למזבח – with a blemish that is not recognized such as a veiled or withered spots in the eye (i.e., cataracts).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:1)


**נָתַן לְאֻמָּנִין לְתַקֵּן.** נָתַן לָהֶם עֵצִים לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּלִי. וּלְאַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה הַכְּלִי קִלְקְלוּהוּ, חַיָּבִים לְשַׁלֵּם דְּמֵי הַכְּלִי וְלֹא דְּמֵי עֵצִים בִּלְבַד:


נתן לאומנין לתקן – he gave them wood to make a tool, and after the tool had been made, he ruined it, he (i.e., the artisan) is liable to pay the cost of the tool and not the cost of the wood alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:2)


**נָתַן לְחָרָשׁ.** הַשְׁתָּא אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן שֶׁאִם נָתַן כְּלִי עָשׂוּי לְאֻמָּן כְּדֵי לְתַקְּנוֹ, וְקִלְקְלוֹ, חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם דְּמֵי הַכְּלִי. וְתָנָא סֵיפָא לְגִלּוּיֵי רֵישָׁא, דְּלָא תֵימָא רֵישָׁא מַיְרֵי בִּכְלִי עָשׂוּי:


נתן לחרש – now it comes to inform us that if he gave the made tool/utensil to the artisan to fix and he ruined it, he is liable to pay the cost of the tool/utensil. And the Mishnah taught the last clause to reveal [more] about the first clause [of the Mishnah], so that you should not say that the first clause is speaking of a formed tool/utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:3:3)


**שִׁדָּה.** עֲגָלָה שֶׁל עֵץ הָעֲשׂוּיָה לְמֶרְכַּב נָשִׁים:


שידה – a wooden wagon that is made to give transport for women.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:1)


**וְהִקְדִּיחָתוֹ יוֹרָה.** שֶׁשְּׂרָפָתוֹ יוֹרָה. שֶׁהִרְתִּיחוֹ יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי:


והקדיחתו יורה – that the dye burned it and caused it to bubble too much.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:2)


**נוֹתֵן לוֹ דְּמֵי צַמְרוֹ.** וְהָכָא לֵיכָּא שְׁבָחָא כְּלָל, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִשְׂרַף לְגַמְרֵי, וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר אִם הַשֶּׁבַח יָתֵר:


נותן לו דמי צמרו – and here, there is no improvement at all, for behold it was burned completely and one doesn’t have to say if it improved more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:3)


**צְבָעוֹ כָאוּר.** כְּמוֹ כָּעוּר. שֶׁצְּבָעוֹ בִּפְסֹלֶת שֶׁל צֶבַע. וּמַזִּיק בְּכַוָּנָה הוּא, לְפִיכָךְ יָדוֹ עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל:


צבאו כאור – like dark/ugly, for they dyed it with the worthless matter/refuse of the dye and damaged it on purpose, therefore, according to everyone, he is at a disadvantage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:4)


**אִם הַשֶּׁבַח.** שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיחַ הַצֶּמֶר:


ואם השבח – that the wool grew in value,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:5)


**יָתֵר עַל הַיְצִיאָה.** שֶׁל צֶבַע. נוֹתֵן לוֹ לַצַּבָּע אֶת הַיְצִיאָה וְלֹא שָׂכָר שָׁלֵם, וּמְקַבֵּל צַמְרוֹ. וּדְמֵי צַמְרוֹ לֹא אָמְרִינַן דְּנֵיתִיב לֵיהּ דְּנִקְנֵי אִיהוּ שְׁבָחָא דְּצֶמֶר, דְּהָא בְּצֶבַע שֶׁהִתְנָה עִמּוֹ צָבַע וְלֵיכָּא שִׁנּוּיָא דְּנִקְנֵי:


יתר על היציאה – greater than the outlay of the wool, he gives to the dyer the outlay but not the entire wages and receives his wool. And the cost of his wool, we don’t say that we give it to him for he caused the wool to receive its increased value , for it was through the wool that he made a condition with him the dyer and there’re is no change that was acquired.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:6)


**לִצְבֹּעַ לוֹ אָדֹם וְכוּ'.** קַנְיֵהּ בְּשִׁנּוּי. לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר לֹא יָהֵיב אֶלָּא דְּמֵי צַמְרוֹ, אוֹ זֶה יִתֵּן שְׂכָרוֹ מִשָּׁלֵם וְיִקַּח הַצֶּמֶר:


לצבוע לו אדום וכו' – it is an acquisition through a change for Rabbi Meir, that he should not give other than the [cost] of the wool, or that he should give him his salary in full and take the wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:4:7)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** דְּקָנֵיס לֵיהּ לְהַאי דְּשִׁנָּה לִהְיוֹת יָדוֹ עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְלֹא נִתְהַנֵּי מִשְּׁבָחָא, וְאַגְרָא נַמִּי לֹא נִשְׁקֹל אֶלָּא יְצִיאָה. וְאִם יְצִיאָה יְתֵרָה עַל הַשֶּׁבַח, יִתֵּן לוֹ הַשֶּׁבַח שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיחַ. וְאִם יִרְצֶה לָתֵת אֶת שְׂכָרוֹ מִשָּׁלֵם, שֶׁשֶּׁבַח יוֹתֵר עַל הַשָּׂכָר, יִתֵּן שְׂכָרוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר וכו' – they fined him for that he changed it so that he is a disadvantage and that he should not benefit from improvement . And the reward also he should not take, but rather the outlay, but if the outlay is greater than the improvement, he should give him the improvement that was made. But if he wants, he may give him his complete salary, for if the improvement is greater than the salary, he should give him his salary. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:1)


**נִשְׁבַּע לוֹ.** עַל שֶׁקֶר, וְהוֹדָה:


נשבע לו – [the thief swore] on the lie [that the had not stolen it] and then admitted to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:2)


**יוֹלִיכֶנּוּ אַחֲרָיו אֲפִלּוּ לְמָדַי.** דְּאֵין לוֹ כַּפָּרָה עַד שֶׁיַּחְזִיר לַנִּגְזָל עַצְמוֹ. דְּגַבֵּי נִשְׁבַּע עַל שֶׁקֶר כְּתִיב (ויקרא ה) לַאֲשֶׁר הוּא לוֹ יִתְּנֶנּוּ:


יוליכנו אחריו אפילו למדי – for he has no atonement until he returns it himself to the person he stole it from, for concerning taking an oath on a lie, it is written (Leviticus 5:24): “He shall pay it to its owner [when he realizes his guilt].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:3)


**לֹא יִתֵּן לֹא לִבְנוֹ.** שֶׁל נִגְזָל. דְּלֹא הָוֵי הֲשָׁבָה עַד דְּמָטֵי לְיָדֵיהּ:


לא יתן לא לבנו – of the person who had been stolen from, for he has no restitution until it reaches his (i.e., the person stolen from) hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:4)


**לִשְׁלִיחַ בֵּית דִּין.** תַּקָּנָה הוּא דְּעַבוּד רַבָּנָן מִפְּנֵי תַּקָּנַת הַשָּׁבִים, שֶׁלֹּא נְחַיְּבֶנּוּ לָזֶה לְהוֹצִיא מָנֶה בְּהוֹצָאַת הַדֶּרֶךְ:


לשליח ב"ד – it is an enactment that was made by the Rabbis because of (see Tractate Gittin, Chapter 5, Mishnah 5) the measure for the benefit of repentant sinners that they would not be liable to this one to spend a Maneh while setting out on the road.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:5:5)


**וְאִם מֵת.** הַנִּגְזָל:


ואם מת – the one who was stolen from.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:6:1)


**חוּץ מִפָּחוֹת מִשָּׁוֶה פְרוּטָה וְכוּ'.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִתְיַקֵּר וְיָשׁוּב עַל שָׁוֶה פְּרוּטָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הַגָּזֵל בְּעֵין אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹלִיכוֹ אַחֲרָיו:


חוץ מפחות ומשוה פרוטה וכו' – and we don’t take into consideration lest it became more valuable and he will retract on the value of a penny, and even one who steals in a discernable manner, it is not necessary to chase after him [to return it].



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:1)


**וְנִשְׁבַּע לוֹ עַל הַחֹמֶשׁ.** שְׁבוּעָה שְׁנִיָּה, שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לוֹ, וְהוֹדָה שֶׁלֹּא נְתָנוֹ:


ונשבע לו על החומש – a second oath that he had given him [the money] but he admitted that they had not given him [that money].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:2)


**הֲרֵי זֶה מְשַׁלֵּם חֹמֶשׁ עַל חֹמֶשׁ.** חֻמְשׁוֹ שֶׁל חֹמֶשׁ, דְּחֹמֶשׁ רִאשׁוֹן נַעֲשֶׂה קֶרֶן:


ה"ז משלם חומש על חומש – a fifth of a fifth for the first fifth became the principal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:3)


**עַד שֶׁיִּתְמַעֵט וְכוּ'.** שֶׁאִם חָזַר וְנָתַן לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ רִאשׁוֹן וְנִשְׁבַּע עַל הַשֵּׁנִי וְהוֹדָה, מְשַׁלֵּם חֻמְשׁוֹ וְחֻמְשׁוֹ שֶׁל חֹמֶשׁ שֵׁנִי, וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם. שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וַחֲמִשִׁתָיו יוֹסֵף עָלָיו, הַתּוֹרָה רִבְּתָה חֲמִישִׁיּוֹת הַרְבֵּה לְקֶרֶן אֶחָד:


עד שיתמעט וכו' – for he retracted and gave him the first one-fifth and took an oath [falsely] on the second fifth, and admitted [that he had lied] and pays that fifth and the fifth of the second fifth, and similarly forever, as it states (Leviticus 5:24): “and add a fifth part to it.” The Torah increased the fifths a great deal for one principal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:4)


**תְּשׂוּמֶת יָד.** הַלְוָאָה:


תשומת יד – a loan


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:5)


**עָשַׁק אֶת עֲמִיתוֹ.** שְׂכַר שָׂכִיר:


עשק את עמיתו – the wages of a hired man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:7:6)


**מְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן.** דְּאֵין חֹמֶשׁ וְאָשָׁם אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הוֹדָה. דִּכְתִיב בְּגֶזֶל הַגֵּר בְּפָרָשַׁת נָשֹׂא, וְהִתְוַדָּה:


משלם קרן – for there is no one-fifth and a guilt offering other than if he admitted as it is written concerning the theft of a convert (Numbers 5:7)“he shall confess the wrong that he has done, etc.” [and] (Numbers 5:8): “If a man has no kinsman to whom restitution can be made, the amount repaid shall go to the LORD for the priest – in addition to the ram of expiation with which expiation is made on his behalf.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:8:1)


**תַּשְׁלוּמֵי כֶפֶל.** וְאִם הוֹדָה מֵעַצְמוֹ אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם כֶּפֶל, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) אֲשֶׁר יַרְשִׁיעוּן אֱלֹהִים, פְּרָט לְמַרְשִׁיעַ אֶת עַצְמוֹ:


תשלומי כפל – if he (i.e. the person who was the deposit holder who stole the deposit) admitted on his own, he does not pay the two-fold restitution , as it is written (Exodus 22:8): “he whom God declares guilt [shall pay double to the other],” excluding one who accuses himself (see Talmud Bava Kamma 84b).



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:1)


**הֲרֵי זֶה מְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן וָחֹמֶשׁ לְבָנָיו אוֹ לְאֶחָיו.** אִם אֵין לוֹ בָּנִים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּנָפְלָה יְרֻשָּׁה קַמֵּיהּ דְּהַאִיךְ, בָּעֵי לְמֶעֱבַד הֲשָׁבָה וְאֵין מְעַכֵּב אֶצְלוֹ אֲפִלּוּ כְּנֶגֶד חֶלְקוֹ. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ה) וְהֵשִׁיב אֶת הַגְּזֵלָה, אֵין לוֹ תַּקָּנָה עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיא גְּזֵלוֹ מִתַּחַת יָדוֹ. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁתְּהֵא הַגְּזֵלָה בְּעֵין, דְּלֹא קַנְיֵהּ בְּשִׁנּוּי:


הרי זה משלם קרן וחומש לבניו או לאחיו – if he has no children, and even though an inheritance fell before him of that one (i.e., who stole from his father and he lied about it), it is required to arrange a return [of the funds] and it should not be detained with him even corresponding to his portion, as it is written (Leviticus 5:23): “[when one has thus sinned and, realizing his guilt, [would restore that which he got through robbery [or fraud, or the deposit entrusted to him, or the lost thing that he had found],” there is no remedy until he removes that which he stole from under his hand and only as long as it the actual stolen object and not something acquired through changing it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:2)


**אֵין לוֹ.** נְכָסִים כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְוַתֵּר עַל חֶלְקוֹ:


אין לו – [he lacks] enough property that he can renounce his share.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:3)


**אוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה.** לְהַפְסִיד חֶלְקוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:4)


**לֹוֶה.** מֵאֲחֵרִים, וּמַחְזִיר אֶת הַגָּזֵל לְאֶחָיו לְקַיֵּם מִצְוַת הֲשָׁבָה:


לוה – [borrow] from others and return the stolen object to his brothers to fulfill the commandment of returning [lost or stolen objects].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:5)


**וּבַעֲלֵי הַחוֹב.** שֶׁלָּוָה זֶה הַגַּזְלָן מֵהֶם:


ובעלי החוב – that the thief borrowed from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:9:6)


**בָּאִים וְנִפְרָעִים.** מִן הַגְּזֵלָה הַזֹּאת הַחֵלֶק שֶׁיֵּשׁ לַגַּזְלָן בָּהּ. וְאִם אֵין יוֹרֵשׁ לְאָבִיו אֶלָּא הוּא, נוֹתֵן הוּא עַצְמוֹ הַגְּזֵלָה לְבַעַל חוֹבוֹ בְּפִרְעוֹן הַחוֹב, וְצָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעוֹ וְלוֹמַר לוֹ זֶה גָּזַל אַבָּא. אוֹ נוֹתֵן עַל דֶּרֶךְ זֶה לִכְתֻבַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ אוֹ לְקֻפַּת הַצְּדָקָה. וּבְכֻלָּן צָרִיךְ שֶׁיּוֹדִיעַ שֶׁזֶּה גֶּזֶל אָבִיו:


באים ונפרעים – from this stolen object the part that the thief has a part in, and if there is no heir to his father other than him, he himself gives the stolen object to the creditor in payment of the obligation and he must announce to him and tell him that this is the stolen thing of [my] father, or he gives it in this manner as part of the Ketubah/marriage contract to his wife or to the charity box, and in all of them, he must admit that this was the stolen object of his father’s.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:10:1)


**וְאִם אֵין לוֹ.** מַה יֹּאכַל, לֹוֶה וְאוֹכֵל. וּבַעֲלֵי חוֹב בָּאִים וְנִפְרָעִים מִן הַיְרֻשָּׁה אֶת חֶלְקוֹ. וְלֹא חֲשִׁיבָא הֲנָאָה מַה שֶּׁפּוֹרֵעַ חוֹבוֹ בְּאוֹתָם מָעוֹת, דְּהָא תְּנַן בִּנְדָרִים [דַּף לג] הַמּוּדָר הֲנָאָה מֵחֲבֵרוֹ פּוֹרֵעַ לוֹ חוֹבוֹ:


ואם אין לו – [if he lacks] what he will eat, he borrows and eats, and the creditors come and recover the loan from his part of the estate. And it is not considered benefit that he pays off his debt with those money, for behold it is taught in the Mishnah in the Tractate Nedarim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2 (Talmud Nedarim 33a) that one who has taken a vow of receiving benefit from his fellow, pays off his debt to him.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:1)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וְאִם אֵין לָאִישׁ גֹּאֵל.** וְאֵין לְךָ אָדָם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁאֵין לוֹ גּוֹאֲלִים לְמַעְלָה עַד יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ אֶלָּא זֶה הַגֵּר שֶׁמֵּת וְאֵין לוֹ יוֹרְשִׁין:


שנאמר ואם אין לאיש גואל – for there is no man in Israel that lacks redeemers above until Jacob our Father, other than this proselyte who dies and has no inheritors.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:2)


**וּמֵת.** הַגַּזְלָן:


ומת – the thief.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:3)


**הַכֶּסֶף יִנָּתֵן לְבָנָיו.** שֶׁל גַּזְלָן. שֶׁכְּבָר זָכָה בָּהֶן מִמִּיתַת הַגֵּר, אֶלָּא דַּהֲשָׁבָה בָּעֵי לְמֶעֱבַד כִּי הֵיכִי דְּתֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ כַּפָּרָה אַשְּׁבוּעֲתֵיהּ, וְהַשְׁתָּא תּוּ לֵיכָּא כַּפָּרָה כֵּיוָן דְּמִית לֵיהּ:


הכסף ינתן לבניו – of the thief, for they already became worthy of them from the death of the proselyte, But now, there is no further atonement since he (i.e., the convert) died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:4)


**וְהָאָשָׁם יִרְעֶה.** כְּדִין אָשָׁם שֶׁמֵּתוּ בְּעָלָיו, דְּקָיְמָא לָן כָּל שֶׁבְּחַטָּאת מֵתָה בְּאָשָׁם רוֹעֶה:


והאשם ירעה – according to the law of the guilt offering whose owners have died, for we hold that where for a sin-offering, it (i.e., the animal) died, with a guilt offering, it must pasture [until it acquires a blemish, thus becoming unfit for sacrifice – and then it is sold, and its money shall fall to the Temple treasury as a donation].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:5)


**עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֵב.** עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם:


עד שיסתאב – until a blemish falls upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:11:6)


**וְיִפְּלוּ דָמָיו לִנְדָבָה.** לְקֵיץ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ לִקְנוֹת מֵהֶם עוֹלוֹת:


ויפלו דמיו לנדבה – for the summer-time of the altar to purchase from them burnt offerings.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:1)


**לְאַנְשֵׁי מִשְׁמָר וּמֵת.** קֹדֶם שֶׁהֵבִיא קָרְבָּן. אֵין יוֹרְשֵׁי הַגַּזְלָן יְכוֹלִים לְהוֹצִיא מִיַּד הַכֹּהֲנִים, מֵאַחַר שֶׁזָּכוּ בָּהֶן כְּבָר:


לאנשי משמר ומת – before he would bring the sacrifice, for the heirs of the robber are not able to take out of the hands of the Kohanim after they have taken possession of it already.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:2)


**לִיהוֹיָרִיב.** הִיא מִשְׁמָרָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מִשְׁמְרוֹת כְּהֻנָּה שֶׁבַּמִּקְדָּשׁ. וְשֶׁל יְדַעְיָה אַחֲרֶיהָ:


ליהויריב – he is the first division of the twenty-four Temple divisions of duty of the priests that are in the Temple and of Jedaiah after him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:3)


**נָתַן.** הַגַּזְלָן אֶת הַכֶּסֶף לִיהוֹיָרִיב בְּמִשְׁמַרְתּוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נָתַן הָאָשָׁם לִידַעְיָה בְּמִשְׁמַרְתּוֹ, יָצָא. כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל, שֶׁהַמֵּבִיא גְּזֵלוֹ עַד שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיא קָרְבַּן אֲשָׁמוֹ, יָצָא. וְזֶה הַמִּשְׁמָר זָכָה בְּשֶׁלּוֹ, וְזֶה בְּשֶׁלּוֹ. אֲבָל נָתַן אָשָׁם לִיהוֹיָרִיב, שֶׁהֵבִיא אֲשָׁמוֹ תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֵבִיא גְּזֵלוֹ וְנָתַן לַמִּשְׁמָר שֶׁלְּאַחֲרָיו, אִם הָאָשָׁם קַיָּם, שֶׁלֹּא הִקְרִיבוּהוּ בְּנֵי יְהוֹיָרִיב, יַקְרִיבֻהוּ בְּנֵי יְדַעְיָה וִיהֵא גְּזֵלוֹ וַאֲשָׁמוֹ לִידַעְיָה. וְאִם אֵין אֲשָׁמוֹ קַיָּם, לֹא יָצָא בָּאָשָׁם שֶׁנָּתַן לִיהוֹיָרִיב, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא נָתַן גְּזֵלוֹ. וְיַחְזֹר וְיָבִיא אָשָׁם אַחֵר:


נתן – the thief gave the money to Jehoiarib in his division of duty and afterwards gave the guilt offering to Jedaiah in his division of duty, he has fulfilled his obligation, as it is explained further, that when he brings his stolen object, even before he brought his guilt offering sacrifice, he fulfilled his religious duty, and this division of duty merited in his [restoration of the stolen object] and that one [in his bringing of the guilt-offering sacrifice]. But, if he gave his guilt offering to Jehoiarib when he brought his guilt offering first and only afterwards brought his stolen object and gave it to the division of duty after that one, if the guilt offering exists – that it was not [yet] sacrificed by the sons of Jehoiarib, the sons of of Jedaiah will sacrifice it and his stolen object and his guilt offering will go to Jedaiah, and if his guilt offering does not exist, he has not fulfilled his guilt offering that he gave to Jehoiarib, since he had not given back his stolen object, and he will have to go back and bring another guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:4)


**נָתַן הַקֶּרֶן.** לַכֹּהֲנִים:


נתן את הקרן – to the Priests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 9:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/9:12:5)


**אֵין הַחֹמֶשׁ מְעַכֵּב.** מִלְּהַקְרִיב אֶת הָאָשָׁם, אִם לֹא נָתַן עֲדַיִן וּלְבַסּוֹף נָתַן:


אין החומש מעכב – from offering the guilt-offering as a sacrifice if he had already given it and at the end gave the [the stolen object].



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:1)


**הַגּוֹזֵל וּמַאֲכִיל.** וְהִנִּיחַ לִפְנֵיהֶם. אוֹ שֶׁהִנִּיחַ לִפְנֵיהֶם הַגְּזֵלָה קַיֶּמֶת:


הגוזל ומאכיל. והניח לפניהם – or he left [as an inheritance the stolen goods] the theft exists.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:2)


**פְּטוּרִים מִלְּשַׁלֵּם.** אִם אֲכָלוּהָ אַחַר מִיתַת אֲבִיהֶם וְלֵיתָא בְּעֵינָהּ, פְּטוּרִים מִלְּשַׁלֵּם, דְּהָא אִינְהוּ לֹא גָּזוּל מִידֵי, וּמִטַּלְטְלֵי לֹא מִשַּׁעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב. וְאִם לֹא אֲכָלוּהָ וְהִיא בְּעֵינָהּ, חַיָּבִים לְהַחֲזִיר:


פטורים לשלם – if they consumed it after the death of their father and there is nothing discernible left, they are exempt from paying. For they had stolen nothing, and movables are not mortgaged to the creditor, but if they had not consumed it and there was something discernable left, they are obligated to return it (i.e., the stolen goods).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:3)


**וְאִם הָיָה דָבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אַחֲרָיוּת.** כְּלוֹמַר וְאִם הִנִּיחַ לָהֶם אֲבִיהֶם דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַחֲרָיוּת, שֶׁהֵם קַרְקָעוֹת, חַיָּבִים לְשַׁלֵּם אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲכָלוּהָ כְּבָר. הָכִי מִתָּרְצָה מַתְנִיתִין בַּגְּמָרָא. וּלְעִנְיַן פְּסַק הֲלָכָה, הַגּוֹזֵל וּמַאֲכִיל אֶת בָּנָיו, בֵּין שֶׁאָכְלוּ לִפְנֵי יֵאוּשׁ בֵּין שֶׁאָכְלוּ לְאַחַר יֵאוּשׁ, חַיָּבִים לְשַׁלֵּם מִמָּמוֹן שֶׁהִנִּיחַ לָהֶם אֲבִיהֶם, בֵּין מִנְּכָסִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם אַחֲרָיוּת בֵּין מִנְּכָסִים שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם אַחֲרָיוּת, דְּהָאִידְנָא מִטַּלְטְלִי מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדִי לְבַעַל חוֹב. וְאִם לֹא הִנִּיחַ הַגַּזְלָן כְּלוּם, אִם אָכְלוּ הַבָּנִים הַגְּזֵלָה לִפְנֵי יֵאוּשׁ, חַיָּבִים לְשַׁלֵּם מִשֶּׁלָּהֶם. וְאִם לְאַחַר יֵאוּשׁ אָכְלוּ, אֵין חַיָּבִים לְשַׁלֵּם מִשֶּׁלָּהֶם אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִנִּיחַ לָהֶם אֲבִיהֶם:


אם היה דבר שיש בו אחריות – meaning to say, if their father left them mortgaged property (or property which may be resorted to in case of non-payment), they are obligated to repay [what was stolen] even though they had already consumed it. This is how our Mishnah is reconciled in the Gemara (Talmud Bava Kamma 111b). But regarding the Halakhic decision, He who steals and feeds his children, whether they consumed it before despair [of the original owner ever recouping what was stolen from him] or whether they consumed it after despair [of the owner] they are obligated to pay back from the monies that their father left them, whether from mortgaged property or whether from that which cannot be resorted to (i.e., movables) and today, movables are mortgaged to the creditor But if the thief did not leave anything [for his children], if the children consumed the stolen goods before despair [of the original owner of ever recouping them], they are obligated to return it from their own [estate]. But if it is after despair [of the original owner] that they consumed it, they are not obligated to pay back from their own [estates] other than if their father had left it for them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:4)


**אֵין פּוֹרְטִין.** אֵין מַחְלִיפִים סְלָעִים בִּפְרוּטוֹת:


אין פורטין – do not exchange Selas for Perutot (i.e., pennies).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:5)


**מִתֵּבַת הַמּוֹכְסִין.** לִטֹּל פְּרוּטוֹת מִתֵּבָתָן שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהּ מְעוֹת הַמֶּכֶס:


מתיבת המוכסין – to take Perutot (i.e. pennies) from treasury [of the publicans] where they place the monies of the taxes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:6)


**וְלֹא מִכִּיס שֶׁל גַּבָּאִים.** גַּבַּאי הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁגּוֹבֶה כֶּסֶף גֻּלְגֹּלֶת וְאַרְנוֹנָא. לְפִי שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל גָּזֵל. וְדַוְקָא בְּמוֹכֵס נָכְרִי אוֹ מוֹכֵס יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁאֵין לוֹ קִצְבָּה, שֶׁלּוֹקֵחַ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא רוֹצֶה. אֲבָל מוֹכֵס יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהֶעֱמִידוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מֶלֶךְ נָכְרִי וְלוֹקֵחַ דָּבָר קָצוּב בְּחָק הַמַּלְכוּת, אֵינוֹ בְּחֶזְקַת גַּזְלָן, וּפוֹרְטִין מִתֵּבָתוֹ. וְלֹא עוֹד אֶלָּא שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַבְרִיחַ מִן הַמֶּכֶס שֶׁלּוֹ, דְּדִינָא דְּמַלְכוּתָא דִּינָא:


ולא מכיס של [גבאים] -the king’s collectors who collect capitation taxes and taxes from crops and other famer’s produces delivered in kind because they are from theft, and especially the heathen custom -collectors or the Israelite custom-collector/publican who is not limited by legal stipulations (see Talmud Bava Kamma 113a), who takes whatever he wants. But an Israelite publican who was appointed even by a heathen king and takes a fixed stipulation according to the law of the kingdom is not under the presumption of being a thief, and one may exchange coins from his treasury and not only this but also, it is forbidden to fell from his taxes, for the law of the land is the law/דינא דמלכותא דינא.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:7)


**מִתּוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ.** שֶׁל מוֹכֵס שֶׁהוּא בְּחֶזְקַת גַּזְלָן:


מתוך ביתו – from the tax collector who is presumed to be a thief.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:1:8)


**אוֹ מִן הַשּׁוּק.** אִם יֵשׁ לוֹ מָעוֹת בְּבֵיתוֹ אוֹ בַּשּׁוּק שֶׁאֵינָם בְּתֵבַת הַמֶּכֶס. וְאִם חַיָּב אָדָם פְּרוּטוֹת לַמּוֹכֵס בַּחֲצִי דִּינָר וְאֵין לוֹ פְּרוּטוֹת. נוֹתֵן לוֹ דִּינָר כֶּסֶף וּמְקַבֵּל מִמֶּנּוּ פְּרוּטוֹת בְּשָׁוֵה חֶצְיוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנּוֹתֵן לוֹ מִתֵּבַת הַמֶּכֶס, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא כְּמַצִּיל מִיָּדוֹ:


או מן השוק – if he has monies in his house or in the market which are not from the tax treasury box, and if a person is liable some pennies to the tax collector from the tax of one-half Dinar and he doesn’t have the Perutot/pennies, one gives him a silver Dinar and he receives from the Perutot equivalent to half its value and even though he gives it to him from the tax treasury box because it is like he rescuing him from his hand.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:1)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ שֶׁלּוֹ.** דְּמִסְתָּמָא נִתְיָאֲשׁוּ הַבְּעָלִים מִיָּד, וּקְנַנְהוּ הַאִיךְ בְּיֵאוּשׁ וְשִׁנּוּי רְשׁוּת:


הרי אלו שלו – for undefined the owners have despaired immediately, and this one acquired it through despair [of the original owner] and the change of domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:2)


**אִם נִתְיָאֲשׁוּ הַבְּעָלִים.** דְּשַׁמְעִינְהוּ דְּאִיַּאֵשׁ, דְּאָמְרֵי וַי לְחֶסְרוֹן כִּיס. אֲבָל סְתָמָא לֹא. וְרֵישָׁא דְּנָטְלוּ לִסְטִים אֶת כְּסוּתוֹ דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּמִסְתָּמָא מִתְיָאֲשׁוּ, מַיְרֵי בְּלִסְטִים יִשְׂרָאֵל, דְּכֵיוָן דְּדַיָּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אָמְרֵי הָבֵא עֵדִים הָבֵא רְאָיָה, מִכִּי נָטְלוּ לוֹ לִסְטִים מִיַּאֵשׁ. וְסֵיפָא בְּלִסְטִים נָכְרִים, שֶׁדַּיָּנֵי נָכְרִים דָּנִין בְּגַאֲוָה וּבִזְרוֹעַ וּבְאֻמְדַּן דַּעַת בְּלֹא עֵדִים וְלֹא רְאָיָה, וְהַנִּגְזָל מֵהֶם לֹא מִיַּאֵשׁ. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי אִי שַׁמְעִינְהוּ דְּאִיַּאוּשׁ אִין, אֲבָל סְתָמָא לֹא:


אם נתיאשו הבעלים – for we learned that regarding despair, when they say, Woe is Me for the loss of money, but not if it is not defined. The first section of the Mishnah [deals with] when the robbers took his clothing, that is undefined, that the owners resign their possession – we are speaking of Jewish thieves, for since the Jewish laws state: bring witnesses, bring proof, for when the robbers take it they (i.e., the owners) despair, but the concluding section of the Mishnah deals with heathen robbers for heathen judges judge with haughtiness and physical might, and when they state an opinion, it is without witnesses and without proof and those who suffered theft from them do not despair. But because of this, if we hear about this, it surely refers to despair, but, undefined, it is not.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:3)


**נָחִיל שֶׁל דְּבוֹרִים.** קִבּוּץ שֶׁנִּקְבָּצִים עִם הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁלָּהֶם:


נחיל של דבורים – a collection [of bees] gathered together with their sovereign.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:4)


**לֹא יָקֹץ אֶת שׂוֹכוֹ.** נִתְיַשְּׁבוּ דְּבוֹרִים [שֶׁלּוֹ] עַל שׂוֹכַת חֲבֵרוֹ, וְיָרֵא לִטְּלָן אֶחָד אֶחָד, שֶׁלֹּא יִבְרְחוּ, לֹא יָקֹץ אֶת הַשּׂוֹכָה כֻּלָּהּ, וַאֲפִלּוּ עַל מְנָת לִתֵּן אֶת הַדָּמִים:


לא יקוץ את השוכה – the bees settled upon the bough of his fellow and was afraid to take one at a time so that they should not flee, he should not cut the branch entirely, and even on condition of giving the money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:2:5)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


רבי ישמעאל וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yishmael.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:3:1)


**הַמַּכִּיר כֵּלָיו וּסְפָרָיו וְכוּ'.** מַתְנִיתִין בְּאָדָם שֶׁאֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לִמְכֹּר כֵּלָיו, דְּכֵיוָן דְּיָצָא לוֹ שֵׁם גְּנֵבָה בָּעִיר, וְאִיכָּא סַהֲדֵי שֶׁאֵלּוּ הַכֵּלִים וְהַסְּפָרִים שֶׁלּוֹ הָיוּ, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא אִיהוּ זַבְּנִינְהוּ:


המכיר כליו וספריו וכו' – Our Mishnah [deals] with a person who is not used to selling his utensils, for since a report of a theft had לוקח gone forth in the city, and there are witnesses that these were his utensils and books, we don’t suspect that perhaps he actually sold them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:3:2)


**יִשָּׁבַע לוֹקֵחַ כַּמָּה נָתַן וְיִטֹּל.** וְיַחְזִיר לוֹ כֵּלָיו. וְלִפְנֵי יֵאוּשׁ קָמַיְרֵי:


נשבע לוקח כמה נתן ויטול – and he should return to him his utensils, and we are speaking about the time before despair.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:4:1)


**אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא שְׂכָרוֹ.** שְׂכַר כְּלִי וּשְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּה:


אין לו אלא שכרו – payment for the utensil and payment for the labor (of preserving the other’s honey).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:4:2)


**שָׁטַף נָהָר חֲמוֹרוֹ.** צְרִיכָא לְמִתְנֵי תְּרֵי בָּבֵי דְּמַתְנִיתִין. דְּאִי תָּנָא רֵישָׁא הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא הָתָם הוּא דְּכִי פָּרֵישׁ יָהֵיב לֵיהּ דְּמֵי כֻּלָּהּ מִשּׁוּם דְּבַיָּדַיִם קָא פָּסֵיד, שֶׁשָּׁפַךְ אֶת יֵינוֹ בַּיָּדַיִם בִּשְׁבִילוֹ, אֲבָל סֵיפָא דְּמִמֵּילָא, אֵימָא אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא שְׂכָרוֹ. וְאִי אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן סֵיפָא, הָכָא הוּא דְּבִסְתָמָא אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא שְׂכָרוֹ מִשּׁוּם דְּמִמֵּילָא, אֲבָל הָתָם דְּבַיָּדַיִם, אֵימָא אֲפִלּוּ בִּסְתָמָא יָהֵיב לֵיהּ דְּמֵי כֻּלָּהּ. צְרִיכָא:


שטף נהר חמורו – it is necessary to teach two segments of our Mishnah, for it (i.e., the Mishnah) had taught [only] the first part, I would think that it is there when he specifies, that they give him the cost of everything, because that which is in his hands, he loses when he spills his wine by his own hands for him. But in the concluding part [of the Mishnah] that of itself, I would say that he has nothing other than his salary/pay , but if we were to teach only the concluding section [of the Mishnah], here when it is undefined, he would have nothing other than his salary/payment of itself, but there (in the first part of the Mishnah dealing with a jar of wine and pitcher of honey) when it is in their hands, I would say, even when undefined, he should give him the cost of everything, Hence it is necessary [to teach both].



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:5:1)


**נְטָלוּהָ מְסִיקִין.** אַנָּסִים גְּזָלוּהָ מִן הַגַּזְלָן. תַּרְגּוּם צְלָצַל, סַקָּאָה. שֶׁהָאַרְבֶּה גַּזְלָן הוּא, שֶׁאוֹכֵל בִּשְׂדוֹת אֲחֵרִים:


נטלוה מסיקין – violent men stole it from the thief. The Aramaic translation of [מסיקין] the shrieking of locusts (possibly cricket – see Talmud Bava Kamma 116b), for the locust is a thief, which eats other’s fields.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:5:2)


**אִם מַכַּת מְדִינָה הִיא.** שֶׁאָנְסוּ קַרְקַע שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים עִם זוֹ:


אם מכח מדינה היא – which took by force the fields of others with this.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:6:1)


**עַל מְנָת לָצֵאת בַּמִּדְבָּר.** לֹא שֶׁיֹּאמַר לוֹ בְּפֵרוּשׁ עַל מְנָת שֶׁתֵּצֵא בַּמִּדְבָּר וְתִפְרְעֵנִי, דְּהָא מִלְּתָא דִּפְשִׁיטָא הִיא. אֶלָּא כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ חֲבֵרוֹ לֶהֱוֵי הַאי פִּקָּדוֹן גַּבָּךְ, דַּאֲנָא לַמִּדְבָּר נָפֵקְנָא. וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אִידָךְ וַאֲנָא נַמִּי לַמִּדְבָּר בָּעֵינָא לְמֵפָק, וְהַשְׁתָּא אִי בָּעֵי לְאַהְדּוֹרִינְהוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר מַהֲדַר לְהוּ:


על מנת לצאת במדבר – not that he should say to him explicitly: “on condition that you go out into the wilderness, and you should repay me”, for this is a simple matter. But even if his fellow said to him: “Let me make this deposit with you, for I am going out in the wilderness.” But the other said to him, “and I also want to go out to the wilderness”, and now, if he wants to restore it to him in the wilderness, he can return it to him.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:7:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ גְּזַלְתִּיךָ וְכוּ'.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁחֲבֵרוֹ טוֹעֲנוֹ בָּרִי גְּזַלְתַּנִי, וְהוּא אוֹמֵר אֱמֶת, גְּזַלְתִּיךָ, אֲבָל אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ אִם הֶחֱזַרְתִּי לְךָ, חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם. אֲבָל אִם חֲבֵרוֹ טוֹעֵן שֶׁמָּא גְּזַלְתַּנִי אוֹ הִלְוֵיתִיךָ, וְהוּא אוֹמֵר אֱמֶת גְּזַלְתִּיךָ אוֹ הִלְוֵיתַנִי וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ אִם הֶחֱזַרְתִּי לְךָ, פָּטוּר מִדִּינֵי אָדָם. וְאִם בָּא לָצֵאת יְדֵי שָׁמַיִם, יְשַׁלֵּם לוֹ:


האומר לחבירו גזלתיך וכו' - and for example, that his fellow claims evidently/with certainty that you stole from me, and he says, it is true that I stole from you. But if he doesn’t know if I returned it to you, he is liable to pay. But if his fellow claims that perhaps you stole from me or I lent you, and he (i.e., the other) states, it is true truthfully, I stole from you or you let me, but he doesn’t know if he restored it (i.e., the lent object), he is exempt from the laws of mankind, and if he wants to fulfill according to the [Laws of] heaven, he should pay him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:7:2)


**אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ אִם גְּזַלְתִּיךָ וְכוּ'‏ פָּטוּר מִלְּשַׁלֵּם.** וּמִיהוּ יִשָּׁבַע שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁחַיָּב לוֹ, דְּלֹא עָדִיף שֶׁמָּא מִבָּרִי, דְּהָא אִי נַמִּי הֲוָה טָעֵין לֵיהּ אֵין לְךָ בְּיָדִי כְּלוּם, הֲוָה מַשְׁבְּעִינַן לֵיהּ שְׁבוּעַת הֶסֵּת:


איני יודע אם גזלתיך וכו' – he is exempt from paying. But however, he should take an oath that he doesn’t know that he is liable to him, for pleading ignorance or offering a possible alternative is not preferable to a certainty, But alternatively, if he would make a claim to him that he doesn’t have in his hand anything, he would have him take the equitable oath. (This is applied, if one who is sued for a debt, denies the latter entirely, in contradistinction to the legal oath which is required when the defendant admits a part of the claim. It being presumed that nobody will go to law unless he has a claim, it is a matter of equity to put the opponent to an oath, to which he may in return put the claimant. See Talmud Shevuot 40b).



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:8:1)


**חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ.** דְּמִכִּי גְּנָבָהּ קָם לֵיהּ בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ, וַהֲשָׁבָה דַּעֲבַד לָאו הֲשָׁבָה הִיא:


חייב באחריותו – for once he stole it, it exists in his domain, and the restoration that he made is not a restoration (without notifying the owner of such).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:8:2)


**וְאִם לֹא יָדְעוּ בוֹ וְכוּ'.** מַתְנִיתִין הָכִי מִתָּרְצָא, חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ בֵּין מָנוּ בֵּין לֹא מָנוּ. אֵימָתַי, בִּזְמַן שֶׁלֹּא יָדְעוּ בּוֹ הַבְּעָלִים בִּגְנֵבָתוֹ וּבַחֲזָרָתוֹ. אֲבָל יָדְעוּ הַבְּעָלִים בִּגְנֵבָתוֹ וּמָנוּ אֶת הַצֹּאן וְהִיא שְׁלֵמָה, פָּטוּר מִלְּשַׁלֵּם. וְהָכִי פֵּרוּשָׁהּ, מֵת אוֹ נִגְנַב הַטָּלֶה לְאַחַר שֶׁהֶחֱזִירוֹ הַגַּנָּב, חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ, בֵּין שֶׁמָּנוּ הַבְּעָלִים הַצֹּאן וְהִיא שְׁלֵמָה בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא מָנוּ הַצֹּאן. אֵימָתַי, בִּזְמַן שֶׁלֹּא יָדְעוּ הַבְּעָלִים בִּגְנֵבַת הַטָּלֶה, שֶׁכָּל צֹאן שֶׁהִיא רְגִילָה לָצֵאת חוּץ, צָרִיךְ לִזָּהֵר בָּהּ בְּיוֹתֵר, וְזוֹ הוֹאִיל וְלֹא יָדְעוּ הַבְּעָלִים בִּגְנֵבָתָהּ וְשֶׁהָיְתָה לְמוּדָה לָצֵאת וְלֹא נִזְהֲרוּ בָּהּ, חַיָּב הַגַּנָּב לְשַׁלֵּם, שֶׁבִּגְרָמָתוֹ נֶאֶבְדָה הַצֹּאן. אֲבָל אִם יָדְעוּ הַבְּעָלִים בִּגְנֵבַת הַטָּלֶה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מָנוּ הַצֹּאן וְנִמְצֵאת שְׁלֵמָה, שֶׁהֻחְזַר הַטָּלֶה הַגָּנוּב, אִם כֵּן כְּבָר יָדְעוּ שֶׁיֵּשׁ צֹאן אַחַת שֶׁלְּמוּדָה לָצֵאת חוּץ, וְהָיָה לָהֶם לְהִזָּהֵר בָּהּ, וְאִם לֹא נִזְהֲרוּ אִינְהוּ דְּאַפְסוּד, וּפָטוּר הַגַּנָּב מִלְּשַׁלֵּם:


ואם לא ידעו בו וכו' – This is how our Mishnah is reconciled, he is responsible for it, whether he counted them or not. When is this case? When they (i.e., the owners) did not know of its theft and its restoration. But if they knew of the theft and counted the sheep and found it (i.e., the number) to be complete, he is exempt from paying. And this is its explanation: if the lamb died or was stolen after the thief returned it, the thief is responsible for it – whether the owners had counted the sheep and it is complete, or whether they had not counted the sheep. When is this the case? When the owners did not know about the theft of the lamb, for any of the sheep that regularly go outside, he must be careful with it even more, and this is the case, for since the owners did not know about its theft or that it was accustomed to leave, and they were not careful with it. The thief is liable to pay as the sheep were lost because of his indirect effect. But, if the owners knew about the theft of the lamb, and afterwards counted the sheep and found it to be complete, as the stolen lamb had been returned. If so, they already know that there is one sheep that regularly leaves outside, and they have to be careful with it. But if they were not careful, they would lose, and the thief is exempt from having to pay.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:1)


**אֵין לוֹקְחִין מִן הָרוֹעִים.** דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר שֶׁמָּא גָּנְבוּ מִצֹּאנוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת הַמָּסוּר לָהֶם:


אין לוקחין מן הרועים – for one can say that perhaps they (i.e., the shepherds) stole – we found that of the owner that was handed over to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:2)


**צֶמֶר בִּיהוּדָה וּפִשְׁתָּן בַּגָּלִיל.** מְלֶאכֶת הַנָּשִׁים הִיא, וְהֵן עַצְמָן עוֹשׂוֹת וּמוֹכְרוֹת. וּלְדַעַת בַּעֲלֵיהֶן הוּא:


צמר ביהודה ופשתן בגליל – it is the labor of women, and they themselves make it and sell it, with the knowledge of their owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:9:3)


**וַעֲגָלִים הָרוֹעִים בַּשָּׁרוֹן.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם שֶׁמְּגַדְּלִין בּוֹ עֲגָלִים. וְשֶׁלָּהֶן הֵן:


ועגלים הרוים בשרון – the name of a place (i.e. Sharon) where they raise calves and they are theirs.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:1)


**מוֹכִין שֶׁהַכּוֹבֵס מוֹצִיא.** מְלַבֵּן הַצֶּמֶר מוֹצִיא מִן הַצֶּמֶר דָּבָר מֻעָט עַל יְדֵי שְׁטִיפָה:


מוכין שהכובס מוציא – from the white matter of the wool, he removes from the wool a small thing through rinsing with cold water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:2)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ שֶׁלּוֹ.** שֶׁאֵין הַבַּעַל [הַבַּיִת] מַקְפִּיד. וְאִם הִקְפִּיד לֹא הָוֵי קְפֵידָא:


הרי אלו שלו – for the [house] holder is not stringent, but if he had been stringent, he did not mind that it should be exactly as he wanted it (i.e., he had no legal objection).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:3)


**וְשֶׁהַסּוֹרֵק מוֹצִיא.** הַסּוֹרֵק אֶת הַצֶּמֶר וְהַמְנַפְּצוֹ, מַה שֶּׁמּוֹצִיא הָוֵי דָּבָר חָשׁוּב וּרְגִילִים לְהַקְפִּיד:


ושהמורק מוציא – He who combs the wool and hatchels it, what he removes is an important matter, and they are regularly strict.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:4)


**הַכּוֹבֵס נוֹטֵל שְׁלֹשָׁה חוּטִים.** דֶּרֶךְ בִּגְדֵי צֶמֶר לְהַנִּיחַ בְּסוֹף אֲרִיגָתָן שְׁלֹשָׁה חוּטִין מִמִּין אַחֵר. וְהַכּוֹבֵס נוֹטְלָן וּמַשְׁוֶה הַבֶּגֶד וּמְיַפֵּהוּ. וְאִם חוּטִין שְׁחוֹרִין הֵם אֲרוּגִים בְּבֶגֶד לָבָן, רַשַּׁאי הַכּוֹבֵס לִטֹּל הַכֹּל, לְפִי שֶׁהַשָּׁחֹר בַּלָּבָן מְגַנֶּה אוֹתוֹ בְּיוֹתֵר:


הכובס נוטל שלשה חוטים – it is the manner of wool garments to leave at the end of their weaving three threads from a different kind. And the washer/launderer takes them and harmonizes the garment and beautifies it. But if there are black threads woven into a white garment, the washer is permitted to take everything because the black in a white [garment] puts it to shame the most.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:5)


**הַחַיָּט שֶׁשִּׁיֵּר מִן הַחוּט כְּדֵי לִתְפֹּר בּוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא כִּמְלֹא אֹרֶךְ הַמַּחַט:


החייט ששייר מן החוט כדי לתפור בו – which is as long as the needle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:6)


**וּמַטְלִית שֶׁהִיא שָׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ.** גָּרְסִינַן. חַיָּט שֶׁהִשְׁוָה אֶת תְּפִירָתוֹ וְקוֹצֵעַ מִמֶּנָּה מַטְלִית קְטַנָּה שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת, חַיָּב לְהַחֲזִיר לְבַעַל הַבֶּגֶד:


והמטלית שהיא שלש על שלש – we have the reading חייט/tailor who evened his stitches and trimmed from it a small patch three fingers by three fingers, he is liable to return it to the owner of the garment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:7)


**מַעֲצָד.** כְּלִי שֶׁמַּחְלִיק בּוֹ הַנַּגָּר אֶת פְּנֵי הַלּוּחַ. וּשְׁפָאִין שֶׁהוּא מוֹצִיא דַּקִּין הֵן:


מעצד – a utensil/tool that a carpenter uses to smooth the face of the board and the chips that he removes are thin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:8)


**כַּשִּׁיל.** קַרְדֹּם. וּמַפִּיל שְׁפָאִין גַּסִּין:


כשיל – a hatchet/spade that removes the large chips.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:9)


**אֵצֶל בַּעַל הַבָּיִת.** כִּשְׂכִיר יוֹם:


אצל בעל הבית – as a day [paid] laborer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Kamma 10:10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Kamma/r/10:10:10)


**אַף הַנְּסֹרֶת.** דַּק דַּק הַיּוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת הַמַּקְדֵּחַ שֶׁהֵם דַּקִּים מְאֹד, שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבָּיִת:


אף הנסורת – extremely thin [sawdust] that is under the borer which are very thin belong to the owner of the house.
